# Denetim Export — Parca 4 (hikaye 151-200 / 200)

## st-0223 — The Monkey and the Crab
Kaynak: PD-uyarlama

### A1
Long ago, a small animal and a bad monkey live near water. The small animal is kind. The monkey is not kind. One day, the small animal find a thing. She finds it on the land. She wants it to grow.

She takes the thing home. She put it in her place. She water it every day. A little plant grow. Soon, the plant have big food. The small animal is very happy. She cannot reach the food.

One morning, the monkey see the plant. He goes up. He eats the best food. The small animal call to him. Please share with me. The monkey make bad things. He put hard food at her.

The small animal is not happy. She goes to her friend. Her friends are a small insect, a little food, and a big thing. They want to help. The insect say, I will go at him. The little food say, I will move out. The big thing say, I will fall on him.

The next day, the friends stay near the house. The monkey come home. He is happy. He makes a sound. He do not see his friends.

The insect go at his head. The monkey make a sound. The little food go at his side. The big thing fall down. It fall on his head. The monkey fall down on the land.

The small animal come out. She says, This is bad. The monkey feel bad. He wants to change. He helps the small animal find food. He share food with everyone. The monkey is kind now. They all live well.

### A2
Long ago, a small animal and a boy lived near the sea. The small animal walked down the ground. The animal was very kind. The other person played games with people. One day, the animal found a red seed. She picked the seed up. She wanted to grow a tree. The animal planted the seed in her garden. She put water on it every day. She sang a song to it. Soon, a little tree grew up. After some years, the tree had big, red food. The animal was happy. She could not reach the food alone. One morning, the person came by the tree. He went up fast and ate the best food. The animal called to him for some food. The person laughed and said no. He threw a hard, green piece of food and hit the animal. The animal was not happy and felt bad. She went to find her friends. Her friends were a fly, a dark seed, and a large machine. They agreed the person was mean. The fly said, I will hit him. The seed said, I will surprise him. The large machine said, I will fall on him. They were going to stop the mean person.

The next day, the friends stayed near the house. The person came home and sang a song. He did not see anyone waiting for him. Suddenly, the fly flew and hit his face. The person tried to hit the fly and missed. Then the seed hit his foot. The large machine fell from the top of the house. It hit his head. The person fell down to the ground. The animal came out and spoke to him. The person felt sorry and promised to be good. He helped pick the food and shared it with everyone. They all lived happily together. They were going to stay good friends from that day.

### B1
Long ago, a small animal and a funny person lived in a village near the sea. The animal walked slowly. She was a good person. The person was quick, but he often played games with others. One day, the animal found a red seed on the beach.

She picked it up. She thought it would grow into a fine tree. The animal took the seed home. She put it in her garden. She gave it water every day. She sang to it in a soft voice. Soon, a little tree began to grow from the seed.

After a few years, the tree bore big, red fruit. The fruit was very sweet. The animal felt happy. But she could not reach the fruit. The tree was too tall. She needed help to pick the fruit from the top.

One morning, the person came by. He saw the tree full of fruit. He climbed up quickly. He started to eat the sweetest ones. The animal called to him. She asked if he would share some with her.

The person laughed. He said the fruit was his now. He threw a hard, green fruit at the animal. He hurt her. The animal felt sad and angry. She did not fight back. She went to her friends to tell them what happened.

Her friends included a small flying animal. They also included a brown part of a tree. Another friend was a heavy kitchen tool. She told them the person was mean. She asked for their help. The small flying animal said he would fly at the face of the person.

The brown part of the tree said he would roll. He would hit the foot of the person. The heavy kitchen tool said he would fall. He would fall on the person from above. The animal thanked them. She explained the plan.

The next day, the friends hid near the house of the person. When the person came home, he was happy. He was singing. He did not see the friends waiting for him.

Suddenly, the small flying animal flew at his face. It hit him. The person cried out. He tried to hit the small flying animal. He missed.

Then the brown part of the tree rolled out. It hit his foot. This made him jump. He almost fell over. Before he could run, the heavy kitchen tool fell from above. It landed on his head. He fell to the ground.

The animal came out. She told him this was what happened when he was mean. The person felt sorry. He promised never to play games like that again. He helped the animal pick the fruit from her tree.

They shared the fruit with all their friends. From that day on, the person was good to everyone. The animal gave him a second chance, and they all lived happily together. The tree continued to grow. It bore more sweet fruit every year. The village enjoyed the fruit and remembered the lesson about being good.

### B2
Long ago, in a quiet place near the sea, lived a small animal and another small animal. The first animal was small and walked slowly, but she was kind and careful. The second animal was quick and smart, but he often played games on others. One day, the first animal found a bright red seed while walking along the beach. She picked it up and thought that this would grow into a fine tree. The animal took the seed home and planted it in her garden. She watered it every day and sang to it softly. Soon, a little tree began to grow, and after a few years, it bore fruit. The fruit was big, red, and looked very sweet. The animal was happy, but she knew she could not reach the fruit alone. One morning, the second animal came by and saw the tree full of fruit. He climbed up quickly and started to eat the sweetest ones. The first animal called to him, asking him to please share some with her. The second animal laughed and said that these were his now. He threw a hard, green fruit at the animal and hurt her shell. The animal was sad and angry, but she did not fight back. She went to her friends—a small bug, a brown seed, and a stone bowl—and told them what happened. They all agreed the second animal was bad and decided to help the first animal teach him a lesson. The bug said that it would bite him when he was not looking. The brown seed added that it would jump out and surprise him. The stone bowl said that it would fall on him from above. The animal thanked them and explained the plan. The next day, the friends hid near the house. When the second animal came home, he was happy and singing. He did not see the bug, the brown seed, or the stone bowl waiting for him.

Suddenly, the bug flew at the second animal and bit his nose. The animal cried out and tried to hit the bug, but he missed. Then the brown seed rolled out and hit his foot, making him jump. Before he could run, the stone bowl fell from the roof and landed on his head. The animal was surprised and fell to the ground, touching his head. The first animal came out and said that this is what happens when you are mean to others. The second animal felt sorry and promised never to play games again. He helped the animal pick the fruit from her tree and shared it with everyone. From that day on, the second animal was kind to all his friends. The animal accepted him, and they all lived happily together. If the second animal had been kind from the start, he would have enjoyed the fruit without any trouble. Which shows that friendship is more important than ego. Every time the sun rose, the trees grew taller and stronger. The local people often watched the garden with great joy. They knew that goodwill always brings good results in the end. Many months passed by, and the seasons changed from spring to summer. The small animal continued to take care of her plants and animals around her. Nobody in the town ever forgot the important lesson which was taught that day. Life went on quietly near the beautiful blue sea. If anyone visits the town today, they can still see the wonderful tree standing proudly in the garden. The story remains a famous part of local history and brings joy to every child who hears it.

### C1
Long ago, in a quiet coastal settlement where the salt spray kissed the coastal vegetation, dwelt two very different creatures. The first was a creature of deliberate, measured steps, possessing a gentle disposition and an infinite patience. Her counterpart was a creature of restless energy, gifted with a sharp mind that he too often employed in malicious pranks. While she moved through the world with mindful care, he darted about seeking whom he might manipulate next. Their paths were destined to cross against the backdrop of an indifferent sea and an unchanging horizon.

One sunlit morning, while she strolled along the damp curve of the beach, her keen eye caught a flash of crimson in the sand. It was a seed, exceptionally bright and perfectly formed, resting quietly among the scattered debris of the tide. Not only did she pick it up with utmost tenderness, but she also perceived within its hard shell the promise of a magnificent tree. She carried her precious discovery back to the sanctuary of her modest garden, cradling it as though it were a fragile secret. There in the fertile earth, she planted the seed with deliberate grace, whispering quiet encouragement to the soil.

Day after day, she tended the spot with unwavering devotion, fetching cool water and singing low, melodic lullabies. Scarcely had the seasons turned twice before a vigorous sapling broke through the surface, stretching upward with eager vitality. As the years dissolved into memory, the tree matured into a magnificent specimen bearing fruit of astonishing size and brilliance. Its globes of red flesh hung heavy from the branches, radiating a sweet fragrance that drifted across the neighborhood. Though she rejoiced in her horticultural triumph, a quiet melancholy settled upon her when she realized her physical limitations.

It was precisely at this moment of vulnerable longing that her mischievous acquaintance happened to pass by the garden wall. Noticing the laden boughs dripping with luscious fruit, his eyes widened with opportunistic greed rather than shared joy. Before she could utter a single word of caution, he had scaled the trunk with agile leaps, plunging straight into the feast. Selecting only the most opulent specimens, he devoured them with reckless abandon while scattering sticky rinds upon the ground. Desperate to salvage some portion of her harvest, she called upward, politely begging him to cast down a share for her.

Instead of showing any measure of remorse, the trickster burst into harsh mockery, claiming the entire harvest as his rightful prize. Only when she persisted did his amusement curdle into malice, prompting him to hurl a heavy, unripe fruit directly at her. The hard projectile struck her protective shell with a dull crack, sending a sharp wave of pain through her weary frame. Stunned and deeply wounded, she retreated into the shadows of her home, nursing both her physical injury and a profound sense of injustice. Yet, rather than surrendering to despair, she resolved to seek justice through the collective solidarity of her truest companions.

She journeyed out to consult her most trusted allies: a resilient little bug, a dormant brown seed, and an old stone bowl. Having listened to the harrowing account with mounting indignation, they unanimously agreed that the arrogant climber deserved a decisive lesson. Scarcely had she finished speaking when the spirited bug volunteered to conceal himself near the hearth and deliver a stinging rebuke. Not to be outdone, the brown seed declared that it would position itself near the threshold to trip the intruder. Finally, the heavy stone bowl rumbled its willingness to tumble from the high roof and administer a definitive blow.

Having finalized their intricate strategy, the conspirators took up their designated positions as the afternoon sun began to wane. The very next evening, the perpetrator returned home in high spirits, whistling a carefree tune without a single suspicion. Little did he know that quiet vengeance awaited him in the warm gloom of the hallway, disguised as ordinary household objects. As he stepped across the threshold, his mind remained entirely occupied with thoughts of his afternoon feast. The stage was set for a reckoning that would permanently alter the balance of power in their small community.

Without a moment of hesitation, the vigilant bug launched himself from the shadows and delivered a sharp bite to the intruder. Startled by the sudden assault, the visitor cried out in shock and swung wildly at the empty air. Before he could regain his balance, the clever brown seed rolled swiftly across the floor and struck his heel with precision. As he stumbled forward in confusion, the heavy stone bowl detached itself from the rafters and descended squarely upon his head. Overwhelmed by the barrage of impacts, he collapsed onto the wooden floorboards, clutching his bruised skull in utter disbelief.

Emerging slowly from the doorway, the garden owner looked down at her humbled tormentor with calm dignity. She reminded him that cruelty inevitably reaps its own bitter reward, while genuine community requires mutual respect and care. Chastened by the experience and aching in every limb, the trickster finally understood the profound error of his ways. Tearfully, he begged for forgiveness and swore an oath never to exploit another creature through selfish trickery again. To prove his sincerity, he immediately set to work helping her harvest the remaining fruit for the entire neighborhood.

From that eventful day onward, a spirit of profound harmony replaced the friction that had previously defined their interactions. No longer did the townspeople witness acts of spite; instead, they observed a touching friendship built upon accountability and shared labor. Had the trickster chosen kindness from the beginning, he might have enjoyed the garden's bounty without enduring such a painful lesson. Every morning as the sun climbed over the blue expanse of the ocean, the legendary tree grew taller and more resilient. Generations of children would later gather beneath its shade, listening to the tale of how justice and friendship triumphed.

The seasons continued their eternal waltz, transforming spring blossoms into summer warmth and autumn gold. Through every shift in the weather, the small animal maintained her peaceful existence, tending her plants with steadfast grace. Nobody within the coastal settlement ever allowed the memory of that transformative afternoon to fade into obscurity. Life flowed onward like the gentle tide, marked by quiet contentment and the comforting murmur of the nearby sea. Even today, if a traveler wanders near the garden, they can still behold the proud branches whispering secrets to the wind.

## st-0224 — Jack and the Beanstalk
Kaynak: PD-uyarlama

### A1
Jack and mother live in a country house. They have no money for food. They have nothing at all. The mother tells Jack what to do now. Jack must take their last animal to market. Jack must sell the animal there. Jack walks to market today.

On the way, Jack meets an old man. The man offers little small things. Jack gives the animal to the man. Jack takes the little things home. The mother sees the small things. She is very mad. She puts the small things outside. Jack goes to sleep.

Night comes. Something big happens. A long thing grows high. It reaches above the clouds. Morning comes. Jack looks out. He sees the long thing. Jack is very willing. He goes up the long thing. He reaches a land. He finds a big house. He walks through the large door.

A person lives there. The big person is very large. Jack stays in a room. The person sleeps. Jack sees money. He takes some money. He runs back to the thing. The person gets up. He follows Jack down the thing. Jack reaches the floor first. He holds a tool. He cuts the long thing down. The person falls away. Jack and his mother live good lives.

### A2
Once upon a time, a young boy lived with his mother. They had no money for food. The mother told her son to sell their last animal at the market. They needed money very much. The boy went on his way. He wanted to find help for his family. Life was hard for them every single day. They had no other way to live well. They were very poor. The boy had a cow. The cow did not give milk anymore.

On the way, the boy met an old man. The man offered special items for the animal. The man showed him some small things. He called them special items. The boy gave the animal away. He took the items home instead. His mother became very bad inside. She threw the items outside on the ground. She was not happy with her son. She wanted real food, not small things. The mother was angry at the boy.

During the night, a huge green plant grew high. It went up into the high air. When the boy opened his eyes in the morning, he looked outside. He saw the huge plant right there. It was very big and large. The boy decided to look closer at the plant. He wanted to see where it went. It was a very unusual thing for him. The plant reached the sky.

The boy went up the long plant into the air. He reached a different land. He found a big house there. He walked inside the large building. He discovered someone big living there. The big person had great power. The boy felt some fear inside. He stayed low so nobody would find him. He looked around the large rooms with care.

The big person was strong, but the boy stayed close. He stayed inside a dark room. While the big person was resting, the boy saw gold money. He took some items and ran back. He went toward the long plant again. He wanted to bring the gold money home. His mother would be happy to see the money. They could buy food with the gold money.

The big person opened his eyes fast. He ran after the boy down the plant. The boy reached the ground first. He cut the green plant down fast. The big person fell down. The boy and his mother were well now. They had enough money. The boy lived happily with his mother until death.

### B1
Once upon a time, a young boy lived with his mother. They lived in a small house near the country. They were very poor. Life was hard because they had no money left for food. The mother told her son to take their last animal to the market. He had to sell it.

On the way, the boy met an old man. The man offered some strange wonderful items for the animal. The boy thought that the items were wonderful. So he gave the animal away and took them home. When his mother saw the small things, she became very angry. She threw them outside into the ground.

During the night, something amazing happened outside while everyone was resting. A huge green plant grew very high into the sky. It reached far above the sky. When the boy woke up in the morning, he looked out the window. He saw the massive plant standing tall.

Being very interested, the boy decided to climb the long plant up into the sky. After a long climb, he reached a strange land and found a big house. He walked inside the large door. He discovered that a tall person lived there. The wild person was very big.

The smart boy hid quietly inside a dark room. While the person was resting, the boy saw gold and valuable items. These items could help his family. He took some valuable things and quickly ran back toward the plant. The tall person woke up and followed the boy down the long plant.

Reaching the bottom first, the boy took a tool and cut the green plant down. The person fell away. And the boy lived happily with his mother for all time. They had many meals now. The family never worried about money again.

The mother was proud of her son. They stayed in their home and enjoyed every single day together. The young boy grew up and helped his mother with everything in the house. Life became quiet and wonderful for them. They always remembered the strange plant that changed their world completely.

Every morning, the sun gave light brightly on their little garden. They looked up at the sky and smiled at each other. Nothing could ever trouble them again after that great journey in the sky. They have found a new life.

The mother has felt happy since that day. If they need food, they will remember their new life. The family has stayed together through every season. The sun shines on their home every afternoon. They talk about the strange journey often. The boy has grown into a good man. His mother has smiled every day. They do not worry about the past now. The small house is full of peace. They know that life is good. The green garden grows tall and strong. Birds fly above the house in the clear sky. The young boy helps his mother with every single task. They share meals together without any trouble. The wonderful plant changed their world forever. They look forward to every new morning. The past hardship is gone completely. They live in peace and quiet now.

### B2
Once upon a time, a young boy lived with his mother in a small house near the country. They were very poor, and life was hard because they had no money left for food. The mother told her son to take their last cow to the market and sell it. On the way, the boy met an old man who offered some strange magic food for the animal. The boy thought that the magic food was wonderful, so he gave the cow away and took them home. When his mother saw the small food, she became very angry and threw them outside into the ground. During the night, something amazing happened outside while everyone was resting. A huge green plant grew very high into the sky, reaching far above the clouds. When the boy woke up in the morning, he looked out the window and saw the massive plant standing tall. Being very curious, the boy decided to climb the long plant up into the clouds. After a long climb, he reached a strange land and found a big house. He walked inside the large door and discovered that a giant lived there. The giant was very big, but the smart boy hid quietly inside a dark room. While the giant was resting, the boy saw gold and valuable items that could help his family. He took some valuable things and quickly ran back toward the plant. The giant woke up and chased the boy down the long plant. Reaching the bottom first, the boy grabbed a tool and cut the green stem down. The giant fell away, and the boy lived happily with his mother forever.

The mother was very glad that the boy was safe from danger. They could use the gold to buy food every single day without any trouble. The little house was no longer poor, and life became much better for them. Many years passed, but they always remembered the wonderful plant that grew so high. People in the country often talked about the strange events of that morning. The boy grew up into a strong man who helped others in the community. Whenever he looked up at the sky, he thought about the giant land above the clouds. His mother would smile and tell him to be careful whenever he went outside. Their story was shared among friends and family for generations to come. Nobody ever forgot how a single small plant changed their lives forever. The family would often sit together by the fire and discuss how much their life had improved since that morning. If the boy had not climbed the plant, they would have remained poor forever. The gold was used to buy many things that made their days much easier and happier. Every single room in the house was filled with light and joy. The mother felt proud of her son because he had shown great strength during the adventure. Even though the giant had been frightening, the smart boy managed to escape safely without any major harm. Neighbors often visited the little house to hear the amazing story again and again. The memory of the giant land remained in their hearts for many long years. They never had to worry about food or money ever again. The wonderful plant had brought a massive change to their world, and peace finally returned to their home.

Many other people came to visit the house after they heard about the gold. The mother always welcomed guests who wanted to listen to the tale. The boy grew wiser as time went on, and he managed the family property well. They bought new animals and built a larger barn near the house. The fields around their home produced green crops every single season. Nobody in the village ever forgot the giant plant that reached the sky. The boy often walked through the green grass and looked up at the clouds. He knew that his life had changed completely because of one single choice. His mother often told him that hard work brings good results in the end. They shared their food with poor neighbors who needed help. The community respected the family for their kindness and generosity. Even though the giant was gone, the story lived on in every heart. Children played near the spot where the great stem had fallen. They imagined what life was like above the high clouds. The mother and son remained together through all the following years. Peace and quiet stayed in their home until the very end of their days. The simple house became a special place where people gathered to hear old stories.

### C1
Once upon a time, a young boy named Jack shared a modest cottage near the countryside with his widowed mother. Destitution had long since settled into their lives, rendering every meal a precarious calculation against approaching hunger. Their final remaining asset of any real value was a milkless cow, whose steady gaze seemed to reflect the quiet despair of the household. Desperate circumstances finally compelled the mother to issue a hard instruction to her son. She told Jack to take the cow to the bustling market and sell it for whatever coin it might fetch. The morning air was crisp as Jack set off along the dusty road, leading the patient animal by a frayed rope. Along the winding path, he crossed paths with an eccentric old man whose eyes gleamed with an unsettling, peculiar intelligence. Instead of offering money, the stranger proposed an extraordinary trade that immediately captured the impressionable boy's imagination. He produced a handful of strange beans, claiming they possessed mysterious qualities far beyond ordinary agriculture. Fascinated by the promise of magic, Jack foolishly traded their last surviving livelihood for the tiny, shimmering objects and hurried home.

Upon his return, Jack displayed his strange acquisition to his mother with a mixture of nervous excitement and naive pride. When the heartbroken woman realized her son had bartered their only cow for a few ordinary-looking legumes, fury overtook her. Not only did she scold him bitterly for his foolishness, but she also hurled the beans through the open window into the damp soil below. Darkness soon fell over the small cottage, bringing with it a profound silence as mother and son retreated to their restless beds. During the deep hours of the night, while the countryside slumbered under a blanket of stars, a profound transformation occurred just outside the kitchen window. The discarded beans did not merely sprout; they erupted from the earth in a violent surge of emerald energy. Driven by some unseen, colossal force, the massive plant twisted upward into the darkness, stretching higher and higher past the sleeping trees. By the time dawn broke, painting the horizon in pale gold, the stalk had vanished entirely into the heavy morning clouds.

Awakening to the pale light of morning, Jack padded sleepily to the window and stared out in utter disbelief at the impossible sight. Standing tall before his eyes was a colossal green pillar, its thick stem ridged like ancient bark and disappearing completely into the upper atmosphere. Curiosity, sharp and irresistible, flared within the boyish heart, overriding any lingering sense of caution or common sense. Without pausing to consult his anxious mother, Jack stepped across the windowsill and seized the rough vine. He began his long, arduous ascent into the misty unknown, leaving the familiar earth far behind him.

Up and up he climbed, his muscles aching and his fingers growing raw against the stubborn bark, until the cottage below dwindled to the size of a speck. The air grew noticeably cooler and thinner as he finally punched through the dense ceiling of clouds into a brilliant, unfamiliar realm. Stretching out before him was a vast, silent landscape dominated by an immense stone fortress whose heavy timber doors loomed like the entrance to a mountain. Drawn forward by a mixture of reckless bravery and childlike wonder, Jack crept cautiously across the threshold and slipped inside the gloomy interior.

The scale of the interior was overwhelmingly vast, designed for a creature of monstrous proportions rather than a human child. While exploring the shadowy chambers, Jack soon realized with a jolt of terror that the owner of this domain was a giant of terrifying stature. Fortunately, the enormous resident was currently fast asleep, his heavy snores shaking the massive stone floor beneath Jack's boots. Spotting a dark, cavernous storage room nearby, the smart boy squeezed himself into the shadows, waiting with bated breath while the giant slumbered on.

From his hiding place, Jack observed the glittering treasures scattered carelessly across the stone floor within arm's reach of the sleeping beast. Heavy sacks of golden coins and gleaming precious artifacts caught the dim light, offering a miraculous solution to his family's desperate poverty. Moving with absolute silence, the boy slipped out from his shelter and gathered a heavy leather pouch brimming with shining gold. Clutching the stolen prize tightly against his chest, he turned and sprinted back toward the open doorway, fleeing desperately toward the waiting beanstalk.

Before Jack could reach the safety of the descent, a thunderous roar echoed through the vast hall, rattling the very stones of the fortress. The giant had awakened, his heavy footsteps shaking the ground as he caught sight of the fleeing intruder and gave immediate chase. Heart hammering against his ribs, Jack threw himself over the edge of the immense green stalk and began a frantic, dizzying descent. Below him, the colossal plant swayed dangerously under the combined weight of the frantic boy and the raging giant roaring down from the clouds.

Reaching the bottom of the garden first, Jack hit the soil running and screamed for his mother to bring a heavy iron tool. Seizing a sharp garden axe with both hands, he brought the heavy blade down with all his might against the tough green stem. With a mighty crash that echoed across the entire valley, the giant beanstalk splintered and fell violently toward the earth. The terrifying giant, caught mid-climb, plummeted downward into the distance, vanishing from their world forever and leaving the countryside in sudden, profound silence.

With the immediate danger past, the mother embraced her son fiercely, weeping tears of profound relief and gratitude for his miraculous survival. The heavy gold coins they recovered from the pouch were more than enough to secure their livelihood for many years to come. No longer did the little house suffer from the gnawing pains of hunger or the crushing weight of hopeless debt. Every single room in the cottage seemed to brighten, filled once again with the comforting warmth of safety and stable routine.

As the years drifted by, Jack matured from a reckless boy into a responsible, hardworking man who managed their expanding property with care. They purchased healthy livestock, built a sturdy new barn, and watched their fertile fields yield abundant green crops season after season. Neighbors from the surrounding village frequently visited the cottage, eager to hear the incredible tale of the clouds and the golden treasure. The mother always welcomed these guests warmly, pouring tea while Jack recounted the adventure with a quiet, reflective smile.

Though the giant stalk was long gone, its legend remained deeply rooted in the collective memory of the local community. Children often played near the fertile patch of soil where the great stem had once stood, pointing upward and imagining kingdoms hidden beyond the vapor. Whenever Jack paused from his labor to gaze up at the sky, he felt a lingering sense of wonder about the vast world above the clouds. He knew that a single impulsive choice had completely altered the trajectory of his existence. Supported by his mother's steady guidance, he lived out his days in peace, forever grateful for the extraordinary morning that changed everything.

## st-0225 — The Green Door
Kaynak: PD-uyarlama

### A1
A young man walks in the big city. It is night. Many people live here. He moves down a way. He sees a sign. It is a door. The door is old. It stands between a house and a shop. The man wants to look. He puts a hand on the door. The door opens. He does not hear any sound.

A light comes from the room. A woman sits by a table. She looks at him. She is very still. The man looks at her. He asks about the door. She tells him about the place. People find good things here. They talk for several hours. They speak about life.

The man feels a good thing. He is very glad. This thing changes his night. Morning comes. He thanks the woman. He leaves the room. He walks back into the city. He thinks about the old house. The night is black. The man is glad. He goes home.

### A2
A young man wanted some fun. He went out during a long night. He walked inside the big city. He walked through many streets. Thousands of people lived there. He moved along a dark road. A sign caught his eye. It was a green door. The door stood between a building and a shop. The man felt very interested. He had never seen such a door. He decided to open it now. His hand touched the door. The door moved fast. A light came from the room. A woman sat by a table. She looked at him with surprise. He stepped across the space. The man smiled at her. He asked about the open door. She told him something important. People without energy could find rest here. They talked for several hours. They spoke about life. They shared different experiences. The man felt a deep feeling. He realized his choice changed everything. Morning came very soon. He thanked her for the time. Then he left the room.

The man walked down the road. He thought about the meeting. The night was still dark. He went back to his home. He never forgot that place. Life felt different now. He wanted to return again. Days passed by very calmly. He remembered the green door. He hoped to see it soon. The city was very big. Many streets looked the same. He walked around looking closely. He wanted that rest back. His heart felt bad sometimes. He kept moving every evening. He looked for the sign. The door did not appear. He stayed strong and full of hope. He knew life goes on. He smiled at the memory. That night stayed with him. He grew up a lot. He learned about himself. He felt ready for tomorrow. He is going to find it again. He will walk down the street. He will look for that green door. He wants to see her again. He will talk to her once more. They are going to share more stories. He believes in the future. He is going to be happy. That night changed his life. He will always remember the room. He is ready for every new day. Life is good for him now.

### B1

Adada took the egg home because he felt curious about it. He placed the egg near his bed and watched it every single day. He wondered what kind of animal might be inside that large shell. The sun shone brightly upon the sand where the discovery was made. Adada often thought about the unknown past of the strange island. He wanted to understand where the object came from and why it was there. Many hours were spent by the man while he waited for something to happen. The days passed slowly, but Adada remained full of hope and patient. He kept the room warm so that the egg would stay safe and protected. Sometimes, he talked softly to the shell as if a friend were listening.

After several weeks, something amazing happened in the house. The shell broke open, and a tiny bird came out. It had long legs and a funny head, which made Adada smile. He decided to name the creature Aepyornis. The little animal looked around the room with big, bright eyes. Adada was amazed by what he saw sitting on the floor. He gave the young creature some fresh water and food. The bird ate quickly because it was in need of food after the long process of breaking open. Adada felt a deep sense of joy as he watched the creature move around. He knew that his life on the island would never be the same again. From that moment forward, the man and the bird shared everything. They spent their mornings together under the warm sun and the bright sky.

As days passed, the bird grew bigger and bigger. They became good friends and walked together near the water. Aepyornis liked to follow Adada everywhere he went on the island. Adada fed the animal every morning with fresh food from the land. The creature ran fast across the green grass and played with sticks. Adada laughed when the large bird jumped into the low sea. They explored many parts of the island that had never been visited before. Every afternoon, they rested under the shade of a tall tree. The friendship between them grew stronger with each passing week. Adada taught the bird various games, and the animal learned very quickly. They understood each other without needing to make any sound. Their bond became an important part of daily life on the remote shore.

However, the bird became too large for the small house. It needed more space to run and play outside. Adada built a big fence so that the creature would stay safe. He used strong wood and heavy rocks to make the new area secure. Aepyornis had enough room to run around whenever it felt full of life. Even though Aepyornis was strong, it remained very gentle with Adada. The animal never tried to break the fence or escape its new home. Instead, it waited patiently by the gate whenever Adada came to visit. The man brought large portions of food to keep his friend healthy and strong. People who might have visited the island would have been surprised by the sight. The enormous bird and the single man lived in total agreement. No other person shared that isolated space with them during those years.

The friendship between the man and the big bird grew deep. They spent many hours together under the sun and the stars. No other person lived on that isolated island with them. Their strange life continued in peace until the very end. Adada often looked back at the day when the egg was found. He realized that finding the creature was the best thing that ever happened to him. The bird grew into a grand adult, respected by the man as an equal. They faced every challenge together as partners in their quiet world. Even when the weather was bad, they stayed close inside the secure shelter. The story of Adada and Aepyornis became a famous tale of friendship. Their unique bond proved that love and care can cross any boundary. They lived happily until their days on the island reached a calm close.

### C1
Adada inhabited a solitary existence upon a remote and strange island where the boundaries of the world felt distant and indistinct. His days drifted forward in an unbroken quiet, shaped entirely by the rhythmic crashing of waves against the volcanic shore. Nothing unusual ever disturbed the heavy silence of the beaches until the morning he discovered the object resting in the pale sand. It was an enormous egg, impossibly heavy, carrying the silent weight of some forgotten, ancient epoch within its smooth shell. Driven by a sudden, irresistible curiosity, Adada hoisted the heavy mass into his arms and carried it toward his modest dwelling.

He placed the strange discovery near his bed, where the gentle warmth of the interior could embrace the cold, ancient shell. Every single day, Adada watched the object with a quiet fascination, wondering what manner of creature might be slumbering inside. The sun shone brightly upon the identical patch of sand where he had first made his remarkable and unexpected find. Often, he would let his thoughts wander into the unknown past of the strange island, trying to piece together its silent history. He desperately wanted to understand where the object came from and why the tides of fate had deposited it upon his shore.

Countless hours were spent by the patient man as he waited endlessly for something significant to alter the quiet status quo. The days passed with agonizing slowness, yet Adada remained completely full of hope and steadfast in his quiet endurance. He meticulously kept the small room warm and dry so that the mysterious egg would stay safe and protected from the elements. Sometimes, in the deepening twilight, he talked softly to the smooth shell as if a close friend were listening in the dark. His voice was a low murmur that blended seamlessly with the continuous rustle of the palm fronds outside his open window.

After several weeks of uninterrupted vigil, a momentous event finally shattered the quiet routine inside the humble house. The thick shell fractured from within, splintering apart to reveal a tiny, awkward bird that blinked against the sudden light. It possessed extraordinarily long legs and a delightfully comical head, a combination that drew a rare, genuine smile from Adada. He decided to name the magnificent creature Aepyornis, sealing their shared destiny with a single, resonant word. The little animal looked around the room with big, bright eyes that seemed to absorb every detail of its new environment.

Adada was utterly amazed by the living miracle that sat blinking on the wooden floor of his solitary home. Moving quickly, he offered the young creature some fresh water and a handful of tender leaves gathered from the nearby trees. The bird ate eagerly, driven by a fierce appetite after the exhausting and arduous process of breaking free from its shell. A profound sense of joy washed over Adada as he watched the clumsy creature navigate the unfamiliar terrain of the room. He knew with absolute certainty that his quiet life on the isolated island would never be the same again.

From that exact moment forward, the man and the growing bird shared every facet of their daily routines. They spent their mornings together under the warm sun and the brilliant sky, walking side by side along the quiet coast. As the weeks drifted by, Aepyornis grew visibly larger, stretching its limbs and testing the limits of its boundless energy. The young animal liked to follow Adada everywhere he went, trotting faithfully behind him across the uneven volcanic terrain. Every single morning, Adada fed his companion with fresh provisions harvested directly from the rich soil of the land.

The creature ran with surprising speed across the green grass, kicking up dust and playing with fallen branches. Adada frequently laughed out loud when the large bird plunged playfully into the shallow, sparkling water of the sea. Together, they explored hidden coves and distant parts of the island that had remained unvisited by human eyes until then. Every afternoon, when the tropical heat reached its peak, they rested comfortably under the deep shade of a tall tree. The friendship connecting the two companions grew steadily stronger with each passing week of shared discovery.

Adada taught the bird various games, and the intelligent animal learned the rules with astonishing speed. They developed a subtle language of gestures and glances, understanding each other completely without needing to make a sound. Their silent bond became the absolute center of daily life on the remote shore, replacing Adada's former loneliness. However, as the months progressed, the bird outgrew the interior dimensions of the small house entirely. It desperately needed more physical space to run, stretch its powerful legs, and play freely under the open sky.

To accommodate his growing friend, Adada set out to construct a large, secure fence enclosing a generous plot of land. He labored for days, using sturdy wood and heavy rocks to ensure the new enclosure was completely safe and robust. Aepyornis now had more than enough room to run around whenever it felt full of life and untamed energy. Even though the bird was exceptionally strong, it remained remarkably gentle and affectionate whenever Adada came to visit. The animal never once attempted to break the wooden barriers or escape the comfortable boundaries of its new home.

Instead, it waited patiently by the wooden gate every morning, watching for the familiar figure of its human companion. Adada always brought large portions of food to keep his colossal friend healthy, vigorous, and strong. If any stray traveler had ever visited the island, they would have been utterly astonished by the surreal sight. The enormous bird and the solitary man lived in total harmony, sharing an unspoken agreement of mutual trust. No other person shared that isolated space with them during those peaceful years of absolute tranquility.

Their strange and beautiful life continued without interruption until the final chapters of their shared journey. Adada frequently looked back at the fateful day when the giant egg had first appeared upon the desolate sand. He realized with deep gratitude that finding the creature was the single best thing that had ever happened to him. The bird eventually reached its full adult size, treated by Adada not as a pet, but as a respected equal. They faced every shifting weather pattern and every changing season together as true partners in their quiet world.

The story of Adada and Aepyornis eventually transcended their isolation, turning into a legendary tale of cross species friendship. Their unique bond proved definitively that genuine care and patience can bridge any divide in the natural world. Even when heavy storms battered the coast, they stayed safely close together inside the secure shelter of the homestead. They lived out their remaining days in perfect peace until their time on the island reached a calm conclusion. Adada never regretted a single moment of the extraordinary life he had built alongside his feathered companion.

## st-0228 — The Time Machine (tek epizod)
Kaynak: PD-uyarlama

### A1
The man sits with friends. He tells a story. He builds a special thing. The thing moves through time. It has old parts. It has other parts. The thing takes people to the future. His friends look closely. He explains the thing. He moves a small part. The room goes around him. Days and nights pass. The thing stops. He steps out into a new world.

The future world has many plants. Little people live there. They have an easy life. They do not work. They do not care about books. Everything is provided for them. The man sees a problem. He looks around every day. He watches the little people. He wants to know more. He stays in the place.

Another group lives under the land. These beings are bad at night. They take other people away. The man tries to help. He works hard. He moves against the bad beings. He stops them. He finds his thing again. He goes into the room. He moves the part. The thing moves back home.

His friends do not believe him. He tells the story again. He knows the story is real. He sits with his friends. He looks at the room. He thinks about the future world. He remembers the little people. He remembers the land under the ground. The friends listen to him. They still do not believe. The man smiles at them.

### A2
The traveler sat before friends. He told a long story. He spent many years building a machine. The machine could move through time. It was made of strong metal. The machine would take any person into the future. His friends listened closely. He pulled a small hand part. The room began to move around him. Days and nights passed quickly. Finally, the machine stopped. He stepped out into a different new world. The future world was filled with green trees. Little people lived a very easy life. They did not care about books or science. However, the traveler noticed a big problem. He walked around the area to see more. The people were small and nice. They smiled at him every day. They played games in the light. They ate food from tall trees. Everything seemed very quiet. Yet, something was wrong. The traveler watched the people closely. They looked worried about the dark. When night came, they ran to good places. They did not want to stay outside. The traveler wanted to understand why. He waited for the dark hours. Another group of people lived under the dark ground. These lower people were bad during the night. They came up to take ground people away. The traveler tried to protect his new friends. After a hard fight, he found his machine. He went inside and pulled the part. The machine moved back through time. He reached his own home. His friends did not believe the story. He will tell them more tomorrow. They might understand the real fact then. He is going to keep building machines. Time travel is a large part of his life. He wants to see the past again soon. His friends looked at him with doubt. The traveler just smiled at them. He knew his trip was real. He sat in his room and rested. His head felt tired from the trip. The room was quiet and still. He looked at the clock on the wall. Many hours had passed. He thought about the future world again. The underground people were very bad. He hoped the little people would be able to live. He decided to prepare for another trip. He needed more things for his work. He went to his shop the next morning. He worked hard all day long. He checked every part of the machine. He wanted everything to be completely good. His friends came to visit him later. They brought food and water for him. They asked him about his big trip. He answered all their questions patiently. He described the green trees and blue sky. He talked about the strange underground animals. His friends listened with great interest. They still thought it was a joke. The traveler did not mind their doubts. He knew the future was waiting for him. He will visit that world again soon. His life belonged to time travel now. He looked forward to the next trip.

### B1
The traveler sat before his friends and told a story about a wonderful journey. He had spent many years building a special machine which could move through time. The device was made of bright metal and small glass pieces. According to him, this machine would take any person into the future. His friends listened closely while he explained how the vehicle worked. He pulled a small metal handle, and the room began to turn around him. Days and nights passed quickly like a fast movement of light. Finally, the machine stopped, and he stepped out into a strange new world. The future world was filled with beautiful green trees and strange little people. Those people lived a very easy life without any serious work or trouble. They did not care about books or science because everything was provided for them. However, the traveler soon noticed that something was deeply wrong. Another group of living things stayed under the dark ground. These lower beings were very dangerous during the night hours. They came up to take the surface people away into the dark. The traveler tried to protect his new friends from those terrible things.

After a difficult struggle, he managed to find his machine again. He quickly jumped inside the seat and pulled the main handle hard. The vehicle moved back through time until it reached his own home. Although his friends did not believe the story, he knew that the journey had been real. He has always remembered the strange world he visited. If he goes back again, he will bring more help. The machine has stayed in his room for many months. He has never told another person the complete truth about the machine. His friends have often asked him about the journey. He has just smiled and said nothing more to them. Perhaps they will understand the truth at that time. Until then, he keeps the machine safe in his house. He sits by the window and thinks about the wonderful green trees. The little people live in that strange world without any care. The lower beings stay under the ground and wait for the night. He must protect the weak people from those dangerous animals. His heart beats fast when he remembers the dark place. He will prepare more tools before he makes the next trip. The room is quiet while he plans the new journey. No one knows what will happen when he starts the engine again. He hopes that his friends will finally see the truth. Until that day arrives, he stays home and keeps the secret safe.

He has thought about the strange place every single day. His friends have often wondered about the truth of his words. If they listen to him, they will learn something important. The journey changed his life in a very deep way. He has planned to make another trip very soon. The machine is ready for the next test. He will check every single part before he starts. The future world waits for him in the dark. He knows that he must be strong and brave. His friends will stay behind while he travels alone. Maybe they will understand after he brings back proof. The room remains quiet as he looks outside the window. The sun goes down and the night begins to fall. He touches the metal handle of his time machine. He remembers the green trees and the little people. He also remembers the lower beings under the ground. He will prepare more food and tools for the trip. Everything must be ready before the engine starts. He hopes for a better result this time. The secret stays safe inside his heart and mind. He will wait until the right moment arrives. Then he will travel into the future once again.

He had spent many years building a special machine which could move through time. The device was made of bright metal and small glass pieces. According to him, this machine would take any person into the future. His friends listened closely while he explained how the vehicle worked. He pulled a small handle, and the room began to spin around him.

### C1
The traveler sat before his skeptical friends and began to recount a truly extraordinary journey through the fourth dimension. For years, working in the quiet isolation of his cluttered study, he had dedicated himself to constructing a very special machine. This intricate device was crafted from gleaming brass, polished ivory, and delicate pieces of crystal glass that caught the amber lamplight. According to his passionate explanation, this remarkable contraption possessed the unprecedented power to transport any willing person far into the future. His bewildered companions listened very closely while he meticulously detailed the mechanical principles behind the vehicle's operation.

With a theatrical flourish, he pulled a small, polished lever, and the familiar walls of the room immediately began to spin around him. A dizzying rush of wind caught at his coat as days and nights dissolved into a single, blinding stream of light. The ticking of the grandfather clock stretched into infinity, merging past and present into a roaring vortex of sensory overload. Finally, the violent motion subsided, the roaring faded into silence, and the machine shuddered to a breathless halt. He stepped down from the metal saddle and cautiously peered out into a remarkably strange, sunlit new world.

This distant future world was completely filled with magnificent green trees, overgrown ruins, and a race of remarkably gentle, small people. Those fragile inhabitants lived a remarkably easy life, completely untouched by the burdens of serious labor, toil, or worry. They spent their golden hours wandering through crumbling palaces, picking sweet fruits, and resting lazily in the warm afternoon sun. Curiously, they did not care about books, history, or science because every comfort and necessity was already provided for them by nature. The traveler watched them with a mixture of fascination and growing unease, noting their childlike innocence and lack of ambition.

However, as the shadows lengthened and twilight painted the landscape in shades of purple and grey, the traveler noticed something deeply wrong. An eerie silence fell over the gentle surface dwellers, replaced by a subtle, creeping dread that suggested hidden dangers. He soon discovered that another entirely different group of creatures lived deep down under the dark, forgotten ground. These pale, subterranean beings were surprisingly strong, aggressive, and fiercely adapted to the pitch-black tunnels of the underworld. Their glowing eyes and powerful limbs made them extremely dangerous during the long and shadowy night hours.

When complete darkness finally swallowed the ruined city, these underground monsters emerged silently from their hidden pits. They roamed the surface with calculated purpose, hunting the helpless surface people who had grown too weak to resist. To his horror, the traveler watched as the nocturnal predators managed to take the gentle surface people away into the dark. A deep sense of moral duty stirred within his chest, compelling him to take immediate action against the injustice. The traveler resolved that he simply could not stand by and watch innocent creatures fall victim to such a cruel fate.

He stepped forward from the shadows and tried bravely to protect his new, fragile friends from those terrible subterranean creatures. Using every ounce of his strength, he pushed the attackers back, trading blows in the oppressive gloom of the ruined courtyard. The struggle was fierce and exhausting, testing his endurance to its absolute limits as the monsters hissed in the dark. After a prolonged and difficult fight, he managed to break free from the grasping hands of his terrifying opponents. Gasping for breath, he scrambled desperately across the uneven stones to find his trusty time machine waiting in the clearing.

He quickly jumped inside the metal seat, his heart hammering wildly against his ribs as the creatures closed in. With trembling fingers, he grabbed the main steering lever and pulled it back as hard as he possibly could. The familiar sensation of dizzying motion returned instantly, sweeping him away from the nightmare landscape of the distant future. The vehicle moved rapidly backward through the corridors of time, reversing the dizzying stream of light and shadow. Finally, the rushing wind died down, and the machine came to a shuddering stop right in his own familiar home.

He tumbled out of the seat and onto the wooden floor, bruised, exhausted, but profoundly relieved to be back. Although his close friends listened to the story with amused disbelief, he knew with absolute certainty that the journey had been entirely real. He had spent many years building a special machine which could move through time, and now he held the proof within his memory. The device stood quietly in the corner of the study, still gleaming faintly under the dust of his return. According to him, this machine would always remain a bridge between what was known and what was yet to come.

His friends smiled politely, thinking the entire tale was nothing more than the product of an exhausted, brilliant imagination. They asked him lighthearted questions about the details, but their tone carried the unmistakable weight of polite skepticism. He did not argue with their doubts, knowing that some truths were simply too vast to be contained in a quiet parlor. He pulled a small handle once more, not to travel, but merely to fold a loose metal plate back into place. The room remained steady this time, yet the memory of the rushing light and the green trees lingered in the air.

Days and nights continued to pass normally in his study, like a fast stream of light flowing steadily toward the future. Finally, the traveler decided that one day he would return to that strange new world to finish what he had started. The future world was still filled with beautiful green trees and those gentle, small people who needed a protector. Those people lived a very easy life without any serious work or trouble, completely unaware of the dark balance beneath them. They did not care about books or science because everything was provided for them, leaving them vulnerable to the shadows.

However, the traveler now knew deeply that something was fundamentally wrong with the fragile peace of that sunny era. Another group of creatures lived under the dark ground, waiting patiently for the sun to set and the shadows to grow long. These lower beings were strong and dangerous during the night hours, possessing a silent cunning that matched their physical power. They came up from the depths to take the surface people away into the dark, repeating a cycle that had lasted for ages. The traveler knew he would have to prepare better if he ever hoped to protect his new friends from those terrible creatures.

After a strong struggle, he had managed to find his machine again, but the adventure was far from finished in his mind. He quickly jumped inside the seat and pulled the main handle hard, testing the mechanisms to ensure they were ready for use. The vehicle moved back through time until it reached his own home, granting him the safety he needed to rest and plan. Although his friends did not believe the story, he knew that the journey had been real, transformative, and entirely life-changing. The traveler sat before his friends once more, smiling quietly as he pondered the endless mysteries waiting quietly in the timestream.

## st-0229 — The First Men in the Moon (tek epizod)
Kaynak: PD-uyarlama

### A1
Two men make a great car. They want to reach the place. The car lands well there. The friends look around. They feel very good. The place is very old. The men take steps. They walk across the land. They move further out. Small little things grow. Little things grow between the rocks. The men see the little things. They feel very different. They decide to take some. Wild sounds come down. The men stop and look. They know something happens. They wait without sound. Light rises from an opening. They see they are here. They hold their head. The great story begins now.

Two men look at the land. They want to find more. They walk every day. They see many rocks. They see small green things. The green things grow well. The men take the things. They put them in the car. The car is very big. They work hard each day. They want to go home. They want to see family. They want to see friends. They put all things up. They get in the car. They start the car up. The car moves off fast. They leave the place behind. They go back to home land. They feel very good. The journey is a win. They tell people the story. Everyone hears the men. The men stay at home. They remember the great journey. They look at the green things. They look at each other. Life goes on for them.

### A2
Two men made a great machine to go. They wanted to reach far up high. The machine landed upon the ground. The men looked around with wonder. They were happy to be there. They wanted to see what would happen. The two friends felt ready for anything. Their long work was over now. They stepped out of the machine. The ground was hard close to them. They took their first steps together. They moved across the wide place.

The space above them was very dark. Light came down on the dark rocks. The friends looked around the area. They saw small green plants grow. The plants grew between the rock pieces. This fact surprised them very much. Nobody expected life to exist here. The men decided to take some items. They put the plants into a box. They wanted to study them later. They checked the area with care. Everything seemed still and quiet.

Then something different happened right there. Strong sounds came from under the ground. The men stopped walking at once. They knew something strange was going on. They waited to see what would appear. Light began to rise from an opening. A large door appeared before them. They realized they were not alone. Their long trip had only just begun. The greatest thing was yet to come. They watched the opening with care. They were ready for the future.

### B1
Two men made a great vehicle to travel into space. They wanted to reach that place and see what was there. After a long flight, the machine finally landed safely upon the ground. The men looked around with wonder at that strange, quiet world. The sky above was very dark. The sun gave light brightly on the hard rocks. Everything was different from life on earth. The two friends took their first steps outside the ship. They walked slowly across the wide surface because their bodies felt light. They had worked hard for this day during many years.

As they moved further from the machine, they noticed small green plants. These plants were growing between the stones on the ground. This fact surprised them greatly since nobody expected life to exist here. The plants seemed to draw strength from the unique environment. The men decided to collect a few samples for further study. They put the plants into a small box with great care. If they find more plants, they will take them back home. The men have seen many wonderful things on this journey. They always talk about how much they love space science.

Suddenly, strange sounds came from deep under the ground. The men stopped walking and looked down at the floor. They knew that something unusual was happening below the surface. Without making much noise, they waited to see what would appear next. Light began to rise from a large opening near their feet. The men realized that they were not alone in this place. They held their breath as the story of this new world slowly opened before them. Their journey had only just begun, and the greatest moment was yet to come. They will remember this day for the rest of their lives.

If the light grows stronger, the men will step back quickly. They have never seen anything like this before in their lives. The friends look at each other with wide eyes and quiet breath. Each man hopes that the thing below will be friendly. They have brought special tools to test the air and ground. If anything dangerous comes out, they will run to their ship. The trip has already given them more information than they expected. They will write a long report for people back on earth. Science will change now because of this single day.

The two men feel very happy about their amazing success. They have traveled further than any other person in history. Their family and friends are waiting for news back on earth. If the machine starts well, they will return home next week. The small plants will grow in a special room on earth. Scientists will study every part of those green leaves. The men know that their names will live on through time. Space travel has changed from a dream into a real fact. The future of people looks bright because of this flight.

They walk around the area to find more interesting things. Every single rock has a different color in the sun. The two friends talk about their past life on earth. They feel glad that the journey was safe and good. If they finish this work, they will feel very proud. The bright stars look down above them in the dark sky. They look at the small plants inside the box again. The green leaves look healthy and strong in the air. They hope to show these plants to many people soon.

### B2
Two men made a great vehicle to travel into space. They wanted to reach the moon and see what was there. After a long flight, the machine finally landed safely upon the ground of the moon. The people looked around with wonder at the strange, quiet world. The sky above was very dark, but the sun shone brightly on the light stones. Everything was different from life on earth. The two friends took their first steps outside the ship to begin their discovery. They walked slowly across the wide surface because their bodies felt light. As they moved further from the machine, they noticed small green plants growing between the stones. This discovery surprised them greatly since nobody expected life to exist here. The plants seemed to draw strength from the unique environment. The men decided to collect a few samples for further study. If the men had stayed at home, they would never have seen this wonder. The machine which carried them was built by brilliant engineers. Every single person who reads this story will understand the strength of those men. When the vehicle touched the dust, a new chapter of human history opened.

Their successful mission proved that people can reach the stars if we try hard enough.

Many years passed after that historic day on the moon. Children read about the mission in their school books every morning. Their fantastic voyage would be remembered and celebrated for countless generations across every continent of their home planet. This stunning discovery served as a permanent reminder that the universe guards its secrets with fierce jealousy. The two friends finally returned to earth wrapped in the hard-earned status of pioneers and modern heroes. Their successful mission proved beyond doubt that humanity can reach the stars if our will is strong enough. Scientists in sterile laboratories continued to analyze the preserved green plants, unlocking secrets of cellular endurance.
