## st-0240 — The Devoted Friend
Kaynak: PD-uyarlama

### A1
Hans is a little man. He has a good place. His friend be Hugh. Hugh be a big man. Hugh talk much about true friend. Hans be good. Hans never feel bad. He thinks friend be important. Hans have a nice garden. He grows many plant there. He likes every green thing. He spend much time outside.

Time come. Hans have no food. Hugh never visit that man then. Hugh take plant without pay. Hans still like Hugh. He be very good to Hugh. Hugh come back to Hans. Hugh want a little thing. Hans have one too. Hugh talk about help. Hugh want much work from Hans. Hans be a kind friend.

Hans agree to work for Hugh. Hans do hard work every day. He feels very bad at night. Hugh ask Hans to help him. Hugh child fall off a thing. Hans run through the night. Hans lose his way in the night. Hans fall into water. The water be very deep. Hans be very tired now. He can not go out. He struggle in the dark.

Hans pass away. Hugh make a big sound. Hugh go home to eat food. Hugh live his life. Hugh do not care much. Hugh stay in his house. The story end here. People remember poor Hans. They know Hugh be bad.

### A2
Once upon a time, Hans lived in a small place. He had a nice garden with beautiful plants. His best friend was a man named Hugh. Hugh often spoke about true love. Hugh visited the garden to take things and plants. He did not pay anything for them. Hans was good and never felt bad. Cold months came, and poor Hans had little food. Hugh did not visit during the cold days. Days came, and the man returned to the garden. Hugh had a broken small car. He wanted Hans to give him his own car. Hans wanted to help his friend. His own car was also broken. Hugh promised old parts to Hans. Hans had to carry a thing of ground food for Hugh. Hans agreed because he believed Hugh. Every day, the man gave Hans hard work. Hans was very tired every night. One late evening, Hugh asked Hans to find a doctor. Hugh child fell off a home animal. Hans was tired, but he ran through the dark air. Hans ran back from town in the dark. He fell into deep water and could not stay well. Poor Hans died in the cold water. At the service, the man made big sounds. Hugh went home to eat a big food.

Hans was always kind to his friend. He gave many things without money. The man took advantage of his good heart. Hugh never helped Hans when things were bad. The small garden was full of life. Hans worked hard from morning until night. The air was often very cold. Hans had no clothes for the night. He tried to stay strong. The man lived in a big house. Hugh had much money and food. Hans had almost nothing to eat. He only wanted to be a good friend. The trip to town was long and dark. The wind moved hard through the trees. Hans ran as fast as he could. He reached the doctor with much care. Then he ran back home again. The water was very deep and dark. Hans could not pass across it. His life ended in the cold water. The town people came to see him off. The man stood at the front. He made big noises. Then he went away to eat food. Love should be about help. Hugh did not understand this real fact.

### B1
Once upon a time, there lived a very kind man named Hans. He had a lovely garden where beautiful flowers grew in the sun. His best friend was a rich man named Hugh. The man often spoke nice words about true love and good work. Hugh frequently visited the garden and took large amounts of fruit. Hans never felt angry because he believed care was very important. When winter came, poor Hans had a hard time with little food. Hugh never visited his friend during those cold months. If winter ends, spring will bring warm days back to the garden. Hans has always loved the sun and the flowers. The cold weather made life difficult for poor Hans. He stayed inside his small house during most of the winter days. He had very little wood to keep a fire burning. Sometimes he felt quite sad and lonely in the dark rooms. He hoped that spring would return very soon to help him. His friend Hugh sat in a warm room with plenty of food. Hugh did not think about poor Hans at all during those weeks. That made the winter even longer and harder for Hans. He waited patiently for the warm sun to shine again. Every day he looked out of his window at the white snow.

When spring finally arrived, the rich man came back to the garden. He asked if Hans could give him his own broken car. Hugh promised to give his old wood in return. Hans agreed because he trusted his good friend completely. Every day, the rich man gave Hans massive tasks around the farm. One late evening, Hugh asked Hans to run for a doctor. The man child had fallen off a horse. Although Hans was very tired, he immediately started running through the storm. If Hans runs fast, he will help the child today. Hans has always helped people when they need him. He wanted to be a good friend to Hugh in every way. The storm was very strong and the wind blew hard against his face. He ran as fast as he could down the dark road. Water covered the ground and made it difficult to see the path. He thought about the child who needed help so much. He did not want to stop for even a single minute. His legs felt very heavy, but he continued to move forward. The rain fell down upon him like cold tears from the sky. He hoped the doctor would be ready to travel with him. He knew that every second was important for the hurt child.

While Hans was running back, he lost his way in the dark. He fell into a deep pool of water. Because he was too tired, poor Hans sadly died. At the gathering, the rich man cried about his best friend. Then Hugh went straight back home to eat a big dinner. If the man had cared, Hans would have lived. Hans has given everything to his friend. Now the story of poor Hans is finished. People came together to remember the kind man who helped everyone. They talked about how much they loved his beautiful flowers. Hugh sat at the table and ate a very large meal. He did not feel any real sadness in his heart. He only pretended to cry in front of the other people. The lovely garden remained empty without its kind owner there. Nature went on just as before under the bright sun. New flowers began to grow slowly in the warm ground. Nobody forgot the story of Hans and his big heart. His memory stayed alive in the hearts of the town people. They learned a hard lesson about true friendship and care. And so the sad tale of poor Hans came to an end.

### B2
Once upon a time, there lived a very kind little man whose name was Hans. He had a lovely garden where beautiful flowers grew in the sun. Although many people visited him, his best friend was a rich man named Hugh. The man was so devoted to Hans that he always spoke nice words about true friendship. Hugh often visited the garden and took large collections of flowers and fruit without paying anything. Hans was so simple and good that he never felt angry about this. He believed that friendship was the most important thing in the world. However, when winter came, poor Hans had a hard time because he had no wood to burn and little food to eat. Hugh never visited his poor friend during the cold winter months. When spring finally arrived, Hugh came back to the garden. He told Hans that he had an old cart which was broken. Hugh asked if Hans could give him his own cart in exchange for the broken one. Hans wanted to help, but he explained that his own cart was broken too. The man, who expected everything for free, promised to give his old wood pieces to Hans. In return, Hans had to carry a heavy package of bread and do a lot of hard work for Hugh. Hans agreed because he trusted his devoted friend completely.

Every day, Hugh gave Hans massive tasks to finish around the big farm. Hans was so tired that he could barely walk home at night. One late evening, Hugh asked Hans to run into town to bring a doctor because his son had fallen off a horse. Although Hans was exhausted, he immediately started running through the dark storm. While Hans was running back from town, he lost his way in the dark and fell into a deep pool of water. Because he was too tired to swim, poor Hans sadly died in the cold water. At the ceremony, Hugh cried loudly and told everyone that he had lost his best friend. After the sad event, Hugh went straight back to his comfortable home to eat a big dinner. Hans was a man who loved his friend with all his heart. He never thought that Hugh would treat him so badly. If Hans had known the truth, he would not have agreed to work so hard. The friendship which Hans valued so much was built on lies. Hugh only wanted to take things from his kind neighbor without giving anything back. When winter weather returned, Hans had suffered greatly in his little house. The wood which he needed was kept by Hugh in a warm storage room. If Hugh had been a true friend, he would have shared his wood and food with Hans. Instead, Hugh let his friend freeze and go without food during the long months. When spring came again, Hugh only thought about his own broken cart.

He wanted Hans to give him a good cart which could be used on the road. Hans was always ready to give whatever he had to anyone who asked him. That is why he accepted the bad trade proposed by the rich man. He carried heavy packages and worked from morning until night without resting. His muscles hurt, and his hands became rough from all the heavy labor. Yet Hans remained happy because he believed in the beauty of friendship. He did not know that Hugh was laughing behind his back. The night when Hans ran through the storm was the last night of his life. He ran as fast as he could because he wanted to save the doctor for the sick boy. The wind blew strongly, and the rain fell heavily from the dark sky. Hans could not see the deep pool of water where he finally met his terrible end. If someone had helped Hans on the road, he might have survived the terrible storm. No one was there to pull him out of the cold water. The next morning, people found his body and brought the sad news to the village. Hugh pretended to be very sad when he heard about the accident. He told the people of the village that Hans was like a brother to him. Then he walked home to enjoy his warm meal while his friend was gone forever. True friendship means caring for others, but Hugh only cared for himself.

### C1
Once upon a time, there lived a very kind little man whose name was Hans. He cultivated a lovely garden where beautiful flowers grew in the warm sun, a sanctuary of vibrant color and gentle fragrance. Although many people visited him, his most devoted companion was a wealthy neighbor named Hugh. The rich man frequently visited the garden, taking large collections of flowers and fruit without ever offering payment in return. Hans was so simple and good-natured that he never felt a single spark of anger about this arrangement.

He firmly believed that true friendship was the most important thing in the world, transcending material wealth. However, when winter arrived with its bitter frost, poor Hans had a remarkably hard time because he possessed no wood to burn and little food to eat. During those freezing months, Hugh never once visited his struggling friend to check on his well-being. When spring finally melted the snow, Hugh returned to the garden, carrying the casual air of someone who had never been away. He informed Hans that he had an old cart which was broken, and asked if Hans could give him his own cart in exchange for the damaged one.

Hans wanted to help his companion, but he gently explained that his own cart was broken too, with a wheel that refused to turn. The rich man, who always expected everything for free, promised to give his old wood pieces to Hans as a generous gesture. In return, however, Hans had to carry a heavy package of bread into the village and perform a considerable amount of hard labor for Hugh. Hans agreed to the terms without hesitation because he trusted his devoted friend completely. He felt grateful that Hugh had remembered him after such a long and lonely winter.

Every single day, Hugh gave Hans massive tasks to finish around the big farm, testing the limits of his physical endurance. Hans worked so strenuously from dawn until dusk that he could barely walk home along the dusty path at night. One late evening, when the shadows had lengthened across the fields, Hugh hurried over with an urgent request. He asked Hans to run into town immediately to fetch a doctor because his young son had fallen off a wild horse. Although Hans was utterly exhausted from a day of heavy labor, he did not hesitate for a second.

He immediately started running through the deepening gloom as a heavy storm began to gather overhead. The wind howled through the trees, whipping the rain across his face while he stumbled along the unfamiliar track. While Hans was running back from town, desperately carrying the promise of medical help, he completely lost his way in the pitch-dark night. In the blinding rain and darkness, he failed to see the danger lurking directly in front of him. He stepped off the edge of the path and fell heavily into a deep pool of water.

Because he was far too tired to swim or even keep his head above the surface, poor Hans sadly drowned in the cold water. The following morning, villagers discovered what remained of the kind little man and brought the devastating news to the surrounding area. At the formal ceremony held in the village churchyard, Hugh cried loudly for all to see and told everyone that he had lost his very best friend. He wiped away genuine-looking tears with a fine linen handkerchief while the neighbors nodded in sympathetic agreement. After the sad event concluded, Hugh went straight back to his comfortable home to eat a large, satisfying dinner.

Hans had been a man who loved his friend with all his heart, never suspecting that Hugh would treat him with such utter disregard. He never imagined for a moment that the wealthy neighbor would exploit his kindness for personal gain. If Hans had known the harsh truth about Hugh's selfish nature, he would never have agreed to work so exhaustingly hard for scraps of wood. The friendship which Hans valued so deeply was unfortunately built entirely upon deceit and convenience. Hugh only wanted to take things from his kind neighbor without ever giving anything of real value back.

When the previous winter weather had returned, Hans had suffered greatly in his little house, shivering beneath thin blankets. The heavy wood which he desperately needed to stay warm was kept locked away by Hugh in a dry, comfortable storage room. If Hugh had truly been a loyal friend, he would surely have shared his abundant wood and surplus food with the freezing man. Instead, Hugh comfortably let his faithful friend freeze and go without proper food during those long, cruel months. When spring finally arrived again, Hugh thought of nothing except his own convenience and his broken cart.

He wanted Hans to hand over a sturdy, reliable cart which could be safely used on the main road for trade. Hans was always ready to give whatever he had to anyone who asked him, possessing a heart incapable of refusal. That is precisely why he accepted the terribly unfair trade proposed by the wealthy man without a single protest. He carried heavy packages across the countryside and worked from morning until night without taking a proper rest. His muscles ached constantly, and his hands became rough and calloused from all the heavy lifting.

Yet, remarkably, Hans remained cheerful because he believed so strongly in the idealized beauty of friendship. He did not know that Hugh was laughing behind his back, mocking his simplicity among other wealthy landowners in the tavern. The night when Hans ran through the violent storm was tragically the last night of his earthly life. He had run as fast as his tired legs could carry him because he genuinely wanted to save the doctor for the injured boy. The wind blew with terrible force, and the rain fell heavily from the black sky, hiding every landmark from view.

Hans could not see the deep pool of water where he finally met his terrible and lonely end. If someone had happened to help Hans on the dark road, he might well have survived the terrifying storm. No one was there to pull him out of the freezing water when he cried out in the darkness. The next morning, people found his lifeless body in the pool and carried the tragic news back to the village. Hugh pretended to be devastated by the accident, wiping his dry eyes before walking home to enjoy a warm meal while his friend was gone forever.

## st-0241 — The Remarkable Rocket
Kaynak: PD-uyarlama

### A1
There is a big thing. He thinks he is very important. A big meeting is coming. The man feel very great. Other people try to be good. The man look down on them. He feels bad because he is great. He believe his water show love. The night show is very big. The man know he is best. Many lights go up high. People watch with big eyes. Other men try to light the man. The man do not take light. His part is not dry from water. Men send him away. He fall into a bad home.

He still try to move. Two children find him next morning. They put him in hand. Children put him on the land. They put fire under him. The man feel very strong. He thinks he will see. He goes up very high. Nobody see him go. He fall down and go away. The man is in the open air. He looks at the night lights. He thinks about his great life. The people look at him. He feels very good now. He knows he is important. The show is very long. The man stay up high. Then he start to go down. He fall into the water. His part is not dry again. He goes down very quick. The day come back again.

### A2
There was once a person. He was a special fire thing. He thought he was better than all other things. He was made for a big event. This made him feel very important. People spoke to him. He answered with a high opinion. He thought his mind was above others. Other fire things tried to be nice. However, the fire thing only looked down. He even began to feel water in his eyes. He thought about how good he truly was. His eyes had real water. He believed this showed his deep nature. The leader son was going to join a special time. A big show of lights was planned for the evening. The fire thing knew his part would be the best event.

The big moment finally arrived. Many lights went up into the dark air. They made good colors. Everyone watched with happy eyes. Workers tried to light the fire thing. He did not want to fail. His thing became cold from his own water. Nothing happened at all. The workers felt very bad. They threw him away into a place near water. The fire thing remained there in the ground. He felt quite bad. He still tried to act important.

Two local boys found him the next morning. They were walking along the water. They thought the old object might be useful. They put him into their box. Later, the boys placed him on the ground. They lit a fire under him to see what would happen. The fire thing was very happy. This was his second chance to show himself. He went up into the air. He flew so fast that nobody saw him go. He fell back down into a field. He made a small sound. He completely went away. He was very glad to move so fast. The morning sun shone on the green field. His long journey finally came to an end. No one ever saw him again after that day.

### B1
There was once a person who felt very important. He believed he was better than all other things. He was made for a special event. This made him feel very important every single day. When people spoke to him, he answered with great strength. Other items tried to be nice. However, the special one only looked down upon them. He even began to cry when he thought about how good he truly was. His tears were real. He believed they showed his special nature. The great king man was getting married. A big show was planned for the town. He knew his part in the event would be the most amazing thing. When the big moment arrived, many bright lights shot up into the dark sky. They made wonderful colors. Everyone watched with happy eyes. However, when workers tried to light the special one, he refused to burn. Because his material had become water from his tears, nothing happened at all. The workers felt very angry. They threw him away into a dark place. The item remained there in the soft ground, still trying to act important. Two local boys found him the next morning while they were walking. They thought the old metal part might be useful. They put him into their small pocket. Later, the boys placed him on the ground and lit a fire under him. The object was very excited because he thought this was his second chance. He rose up into the air. He flew so fast nobody saw him. He fell back down into a field. He made a tiny sound and completely disappeared.

Many days passed after that strange morning. People talked about the big show for a long time. Children played games near the field where the item had fallen. They searched for any sign of the lost thing. No one ever found the small piece again. The story became a lesson for everyone in the local community. If you think too much about your own power, you might lose everything. True friends always stay quiet and kind. They do not look down upon other people. Life is short, so we should enjoy every single moment. We have learned that this often brings a bad end. Now, the town is quiet and calm. Children grow up knowing the old story by heart. They understand that being quiet is much better than being proud. Every year, people remember the strange event with a smile. They tell the story to new visitors who come to the town. The memory remains alive in their hearts now. Nothing can change the past now. We must always care for one another. That is the best way to live a good life. If you are good to others, you will find joy. People have shared this lesson through many generations. We must keep these thoughts in mind every single day. The sun will rise again, and the world will move forward. Every person can learn from the past.

### B2
There was once a very proud light who believed that he was better than all other lights. He was made for a royal wedding, which made him feel extremely important. When people spoke to him, he answered with great pride because he thought his mind was very high. Other lights tried to be nice, but the light only looked down upon them. He even began to cry when he thought about how wonderful he truly was. His tears were real, but he believed they showed how deeply he cared about his own brilliant nature. The king son was getting married, and a grand show of lights was planned for the evening. The light knew that his part in the celebration would be the most amazing event of the night.

When the big moment finally arrived, many bright lights shot up into the dark sky. They made wonderful colors that everyone watched with happy eyes. However, when workers tried to light the proud light, he refused to burn properly. Because his liquid had become wet from his own silly tears, nothing happened at all. The workers felt very angry and threw him away into a dark hole near a small stream. The light remained there in the soft ground, feeling quite sad though he still tried to act important. Two local boys found him the next morning while they were walking along the water. They thought the old metal tube might be useful for a small game, so they put him into their pocket.

Later, the boys placed him on the ground and lit a fire beneath him to see what would happen. The light was very excited because he thought this was his second chance to shine. He rose up into the air, but he flew so fast that nobody even saw him go. He fell back down into a field, made a tiny sound, and completely went away. Many people remember the story of the proud light who failed during the royal wedding. If his liquid had not become wet, he would have shone brightly across the sky. Instead, he spent his time in the ground before the boys found him by the water.

Everything that happened to him taught a lesson about pride and how people should behave. He always wanted to be the center of attention, but he only ended up in a small field. Perhaps if he had been nice to others, his life would have taken a different path. We can learn from his mistakes and try to remain small no matter how important we feel. Life is full of moments where our choices define who we truly are in the end. The royal wedding was an event that many generations would discuss for a long time. Guests came from all over the country to see the wonderful celebration and the bright shows.

Every worker worked hard to ensure that everything went according to the strict plan. When the light failed, the crowd was quite surprised by the strange silence. Workers usually check every detail before they start any important show. However, nobody could predict that the light would cry and ruin his own chance to shine. The little boys who found him later had no idea about his royal background. They just saw a metal object that might be fun to play with during their morning walk. After the boys placed him on the ground, the final moment of the light finally came.

He shot up into the air so fast that people could barely follow his movement. His journey ended quickly when he fell back down into the open grass. Nobody heard him make any significant sound as he went away from sight. The story remains a classic example of how pride often leads to a strange fall. Even today, children and adults both can enjoy the tale of the light and his tears. We should always remember to treat everyone with respect and care every single day. That way, we can avoid the same result as the proud light who thought he was better than everyone else.

### C1
There was once a remarkably haughty pyrotechnic device who harbored the absolute conviction that he was vastly superior to all other illuminations in the realm. Fashioned expressly for a royal wedding, he felt an intoxicating sense of importance pulsing through his metallic core. Whenever lesser candles or lanterns spoke to him, he would reply with an air of profound condescension, utterly convinced of his superior intellect. Other lights occasionally attempted to cultivate a pleasant conversation, but the arrogant cylinder consistently looked down upon their humble flickers. So moved was he by his own imagined magnificence that he would frequently weep gentle tears of pure admiration for his own brilliant nature. Though his moisture was entirely genuine, he convinced himself that these emotional drops were proof of his deep sensitivity and noble spirit.

The young prince of the kingdom was preparing to take his bride, and a magnificent display of illumination was meticulously planned for the evening festivities. The proud rocket knew without a single doubt that his grand finale would eclipse every other event of the evening. He envisioned himself soaring into the velvety darkness, leaving the entire court gasping at his unprecedented radiance. Every worker labored tirelessly behind the scenes to ensure that every detail of the strict schedule proceeded without a flaw. When the crucial moment finally arrived, a chorus of brilliant lights shot up into the dark sky with breathtaking velocity. They burst into wondrous cascades of color that every spectator watched with wide, happy eyes.

However, when the technicians finally attempted to ignite the proud rocket, he stubbornly refused to cooperate or burn properly. Because his internal composition had become dampened by his own excessive and foolish tears, nothing happened at all. The spark hissed briefly against the damp paper and vanished, leaving the cylinder completely inert. Overcome by sudden fury at the ruined schedule, the workers felt deeply angry and hurled the heavy tube away. It fell unceremoniously into a dark, muddy hole near a small, babbling stream. The light remained half-buried in the soft ground, feeling quite miserable yet still attempting to maintain his grand posture.

The following morning, two local boys happened to walk along the winding water while searching for adventure. Their sharp eyes soon discovered the discarded metal tube resting quietly in the damp earth. Believing that the cylindrical object might prove useful for a small game they were inventing, they reached down and retrieved it. Without any inkling of its royal background, they slipped the heavy metal casing directly into their pocket. They continued their morning walk, quite satisfied with their curious little treasure and eager to test its capabilities. The rocket, trapped inside the dark fabric of the pocket, listened to their muffled voices with a renewed surge of vanity.

Later in the day, the boys placed the damp tube upright on the open ground and carefully built a small fire beneath it. They wanted to see what kind of reaction the mysterious object might produce. The rocket felt a sudden jolt of intense excitement, fully convinced that this was his long-awaited second chance to shine. He puffed his chest out metaphorically, preparing for an ascent that would surely astonish the entire countryside. With a sudden hiss of pent-up energy, he shot straight up into the air with astonishing speed. His departure was so rapid that nobody standing nearby even managed to catch a glimpse of his trajectory.

He climbed far higher than anyone could have anticipated, piercing the upper air before gravity finally recalled him. He tumbled headfirst back down toward the earth, landing heavily in a quiet, empty field. Upon impact, he made a tiny, pathetic sound and completely sputtered out of existence. No grand applause greeted his arrival, and not a single spectator watched his descent from the heavens. The magnificent promise of his royal debut had ended in absolute obscurity among the quiet grass. His brief, blinding career was finished forever in a matter of mere seconds.

Many people across the kingdom continued to remember the strange story of the proud light who failed during the royal celebration. If only his internal components had not been spoiled by moisture, he would have truly shone brilliantly across the evening sky. Instead, he spent his humiliating hours hiding in the damp ground before the children found him by the water. Everything that happened to him offered a clear lesson about the dangers of excessive vanity and improper behavior. He had desperately wanted to remain the absolute center of attention at every possible moment. Yet his stubborn obsession with his own importance only delivered him to an anonymous field.

Had he chosen to treat others with kindness and humility, his life story might have taken a vastly different path. We can all learn valuable lessons from his ridiculous mistakes and try to remain modest no matter how important we feel. Human existence is constantly filled with defining moments where our choices reveal who we truly are beneath the surface. The royal wedding itself remained a legendary event that generations of townspeople would discuss for many decades. Guests had traveled from distant provinces just to witness the grand celebration and the dazzling shows. Every laborer had worked tirelessly to guarantee that the elaborate schedule unfolded according to plan.

When the proud light malfunctioned, the surrounding crowd had been genuinely surprised by the sudden, awkward silence. Experienced technicians usually inspect every single component with extreme care before launching any ambitious exhibition. However, no professional could possibly have predicted that a pyrotechnic device would ruin its own performance through sheer vanity. The little boys who discovered him by the stream had no concept of his grand origins or his failed destiny. They simply recognized a discarded piece of metal that might provide amusement during their morning excursion. When they finally set him upon the earth and applied the flame, the final chapter of his existence began.

He had shot upward with such tremendous velocity that onlookers could barely follow his motion through the air. His grand journey concluded abruptly as he plummeted back into the open grass without an audience. Scarcely anyone heard him make a significant sound as he vanished permanently from the visual field. This unfortunate tale remains a classic example of how unchecked arrogance consistently paves the way for a peculiar fall. To this day, both children and adults can reflect upon the cautionary tale of the weeping light. We must continually remind ourselves to treat every person with genuine respect and thoughtful consideration.

By practicing humility in our daily interactions, we can easily avoid sharing the miserable fate of the boastful rocket. He had truly believed that his superior intellect set him apart from every other creation in the workshop. Yet his refusal to connect with those around him guaranteed his eventual downfall in the mud. True worth is rarely found in loud boasts or manufactured tears of self-admiration. Instead, it emerges quietly through kindness, patience, and a willingness to stand shoulder to shoulder with others. Let us remember the lesson of the field and choose a path of grace over the empty pursuit of pride.

## st-0242 — The Star-Child (tek epizod)
Kaynak: PD-uyarlama

### A1
Once, people find a little child. They find the child in forest. The child has no money. The child has no family. They bring him to a house. A man raises the little child. The child grows up very big. He is bad to other people. He thinks he is very good. He is not kind at all. He does not help anyone. He looks down at people. He says bad words to children. He makes other children feel small.

One day, a woman comes here. She says she is his mother. The man says no to her. He is very mean to her. He is very bad to her. His face changes right now. He looks like something bad. He looks like water things. He sees his face in water. He knows his work is bad. He wants to find his mother. He wants to say thank you now.

He walks across the world. He goes day after day. He tries to help people. He helps other people now. His mother comes to him. He sees his mother again. His good face returns to him. He is a good person now. He walks every day. He helps every child he sees. He gives food to people. He is a good man now.

### A2
Once upon a time, men found a baby boy in a forest. The night was cold. The child had white clothes and a face like a star. They brought him home. The man raised the child as his own son. The star-child grew up. He became very high and strong. He was bad to other people. He thought he was better than everyone else. He laughed at poor children and animals. He treated them with total lack of respect. He did not care about anyone else. He thought only about himself. Days passed by at a low pace. The star-child lived like a boss in the house. He walked around the village every morning. He showed no love to the people. The town people did not like his bad behavior.

One day, a poor woman came to the village. She looked for her lost child. She claim the star-child was her son. He did not accept her. He treated her with bad issues and words. He said she was too small to be his mother. His face changed immediately. His face became like a fish and a small animal. He saw himself in a water place. He realize his behavior had been bad. He decide to walk across the world. He wanted to find his mother and earn her love. He was going to travel far away. The people watched him leave the village. They did not say anything to him.

He suffer greatly during his long travel. He learned how to be kind to everyone. He helped animals in trouble. He shared his food with the poor. He walked through many cities and towns. He met different people along the road. He gave his money to those in need. He slept on the ground at night. His heart became nice and good. He always thought about his lovely mother. He hoped to see her face again. He was going to find her soon.

Finally, his mother appeared. His true face returned because his heart was finally good. He holds her close. She smiled at her son. They were very happy together. They went back home. He lived happily after that day. He never forgot his hard work. He always helped the poor and the small. The village people loved him again.

### B1
Once upon a time, men found a little baby boy inside a forest. This happened during a cold night. The child was dressed in gold clothes and had a face like a star. They brought him home, and the man raised him as his own son. Many years passed by while the boy grew older and bigger every single day. He had everything he could ever want in life, and people loved his looks. They always told him that he was the most beautiful child in the entire world. The boy believed these words and thought he was better than anyone else around him.

As the star-child grew up, he became very proud and hard to other people. He thought that he was better than everyone else because of his beauty. He laughed at poor children and animals, treating them with total bad behavior. He never showed any respect to anyone who did not look as fine as him. His heart became cold, and he did not care about anybody else in the town.

One day, a poor woman came to the village looking for her lost child. She claimed that the star-child was her son, but he refused to accept her. He treated her with terrible force, saying she was too bad to be his mother. He shouted at her and told her to leave the village right away. The poor woman cried and walked away into the dark forest with a broken heart.

Immediately, his beauty changed, and his face became like that of a fish and a bird. When he saw himself in a pool of water, he realized something. He realized how bad his behavior had been. He saw his new face and felt terrible pain deep inside his mind and soul. He decided to walk across the world until he found his mother and earned her love. He knew that he must change his ways if he wanted to fix his life.

During his long journey, he suffered greatly and learned how to be kind to everyone. He helped animals in trouble and shared his food with the poor people. He walked through many towns and forests while looking everywhere for his mother. Many months passed, and he always tried to do good things for other people. He finally understood why his face had changed and why his heart was wrong.

Finally, his mother appeared, and his true beauty returned because his heart was finally good. She held him and told him that she gave him back his place. She spoke sweet words to him and loved him completely. The young man smiled and thanked her with tears running down his face. He lived a long and happy life, always helping people and being kind to everyone. Everyone in the village respected him because he had become a truly good person.

### B2
Once upon a time, several kind people found a baby boy inside a big forest during a cold night. The child was wrapped in gold clothes and had a face like a star. They brought him home, and the good man raised him as his own son. As the star child grew up, he became very proud and very mean to other people. He thought that he was better than everyone else because of his beauty. He laughed at poor children and animals, treating them with total bad action.

One day, a poor woman came to the village looking for her lost child. She claimed that the star child was her son, but he refused to accept her. He treated her with terrible anger, saying she was too ugly to be his mother. Immediately, his beauty changed, and his face became like that of a small water animal and a green jump bug. When he saw himself in a pool of water, he realized how bad his behavior had been. He decided to walk across the world until he found his mother and earned her love.

During his long journey, he suffered greatly and learned how to be good to everyone. He helped animals in trouble and shared his food with the poor. Finally, his mother appeared, and his true beauty returned because his heart was finally good. He lived happily after that day, always helping anyone who needed support. Many years passed while he traveled across mountains and rivers to help people. Every person who met him felt the heat of his new heart.

The world was full of challenges, but he never stopped being good to others. He understood that true beauty comes from a pure and kind heart. Animals and people too respected him because he always shared what he had. His mother was proud of the man that her son had become. They lived together in a small house near the edge of the forest. Whenever someone was sad, he would bring them food and warm clothes.

His life changed completely after he learned the true meaning of love. No one ever saw him act with pride or anger again. The children of the village loved playing games with him every afternoon. He taught them that being good is more important than physical beauty. Spring came and went, bringing new life to the trees and flowers. Birds sang lovely songs in the morning while the sun shone brightly above.

He looked out at the green grass and thanked the world for his new start. Nothing could ever break the strong bond between him and his mother. They spent many quiet years together in that quiet place. His heart remained full of love until the end of his days. People remembered his story for generations as a lesson in small and sweet feeling. He always remembered to be nice to everyone he met along the road. The sun shone down upon the green grass every single day. He walked through the trees and looked at the beautiful sky above him. Many people came from other towns just to see him and talk with him. He gave them food and helped them whenever they had a problem. His life was full of peace and quiet joy in the forest. The birds sang sweet songs while the wind moved through the leaves. He knew that love was the most important thing in the world. His mother smiled at him because she was so happy. They shared everything they had with everyone who came to their home. The children played near the water and laughed without any fear. He watched them and felt a deep peace inside his heart. Nothing could ever take away the joy that they shared together. The days passed quietly as they lived in peace. He always remembered the lesson that he had learned so long ago. His hands were strong, and he used them to help people every day. The world was a better place because he decided to change his life.

### C1
It was upon a bitter winter midnight that several woodcutters stumbled across an abandoned infant swathed in garments of woven gold. The biting frost nipped at the pine branches, yet the child lay untouched by the cold, possessing a countenance as luminous as a fallen star. Moved by pity, the poorest of these men took the foundling home to his meager hut and reared him as his very own. As the Star-Child matured into boyhood, his physical perfection became a marvel throughout the surrounding countryside. Yet beneath his radiant exterior lay a spirit hardened by vanity and inflated by his own incomparable beauty. He fancied himself superior to all mortals, disdaining those whose features lacked the celestial symmetry of his own. Cruelty soon became his preferred pastime, for he derived a malicious pleasure from tormenting the vulnerable. He mocked the beggar children who wandered near the village lanes, pelting them with sharp stones and laughing at their rags. Not even the helpless creatures of the forest escaped his torment, as he trapped birds and bruised animals merely to watch them suffer. His adoptive father often remonstrated with him, pleading for gentleness and humility, but the boy merely tossed his golden curls in haughty dismissal. He believed that beauty was the only true virtue in the world and that ugliness was an unforgivable crime. Never once did he suspect that the soul dwelling within his faultless frame was rapidly decaying into shadow.

One afternoon, as the villagers gathered by the marketplace, a wretched beggar woman limped into their midst with torn shoes and bleeding feet. She had traveled vast distances across perilous mountains, driven solely by the desperate hope of reclaiming her long-lost son. Catching sight of the proud Star-Child standing amidst his jeering companions, she cried out and stumbled toward him with open arms. She claimed him as the infant stolen from her bosom years ago during a terrible tempest in the forest. Instead of rushing to her embrace, the boy recoiled in bitter disgust, his face twisting into a snarl of cruel mockery. He denied her entirely, declaring that he would rather perish than acknowledge such a filthy, repulsive creature as his mother.

He spat harsh insults upon her poverty, demanding that she vanish from his sight forever and never return to shame him. The wretched woman wept bitter tears, turning away into the deepening twilight with a heart already shattered beyond repair. Scarcely had the cruel words left his lips when a terrifying transformation seized his arrogant form. He caught his reflection in a quiet forest pool and gasped aloud in sudden, agonizing horror. His skin had turned green and scaly like that of a water toad, and his face was bloated and hideous. The radiant beauty that had once inspired such boundless pride had vanished completely in a single dreadful moment.

When the cruel youth realized the true measure of his wickedness, remorse struck him like a physical blow. He understood that his outward ugliness was merely the mirror of his inner soul, blackened by arrogance and malice. Weeping bitterly, he resolved to wander across the wide earth until he found his rejected mother and won her forgiveness. Day after day, he searched through dense thickets and over jagged mountain ridges, calling her name to the empty winds. The harsh thorns tore his hands, and the stony paths bruised his bare feet, yet he refused to yield to despair. Every hardship he endured served as a quiet lesson in humility, slowly eroding the stubborn pride that had ruined his life.

During his solitary pilgrimage, he encountered numerous creatures in distress and eagerly offered them whatever meager assistance he could provide. He freed trapped moles from tangled roots and shared his crusts of dry bread with starving winter birds. When weary travelers collapsed by the roadside, he used his own tattered cloak to shield them from the biting wind. The animals, once terrified of his cruel presence, now gathered trustingly around him as he spoke words of gentle comfort. Slowly, almost imperceptibly, his tortured features began to soften under the influence of unselfish compassion. He learned to value the quiet joy of serving others far above the empty praise of the proud.

Many long years slipped away while he journeyed through distant lands, never faltering in his mission of redemption. One evening, as he entered a strange village, he found guards beating a poor beggar with iron rods. Without a moment of hesitation, he threw himself between the guards and the helpless victim, offering his own back to the blows. Just as he embraced the suffering stranger, his mother stepped forward from the crowd and laid a trembling hand upon his shoulder. She looked into his weary eyes with infinite tenderness and whispered words of complete forgiveness. At that very instant, the cruel curse dissolved away, and his radiant star-like beauty returned in full splendor.

The warmth of a renewed heart radiated from him, touching every person who crossed his path with unexpected grace. He returned to the small cottage near the edge of the forest, accompanied by the mother he had so dearly missed. Together they lived in quiet harmony, sharing their modest provisions with anyone who sought shelter beneath their roof. The villagers welcomed them with profound affection, marveling at the gentle transformation that had redeemed the once-hateful boy. Children gathered near the sparkling stream every afternoon to play games of tag and listen to his wondrous stories. He taught them that true nobility resides solely within the quiet chambers of a compassionate heart.

Spring returned each year with vibrant green grass and countless wildflowers blooming across the sunlit meadows. Sweet melodies echoed from the branches as nesting birds greeted the golden dawn with cheerful morning songs. The wind whispered softly through the ancient leaves, carrying an atmosphere of deep and abiding peace. Nothing could ever fracture the unbreakable bond that united the son and his devoted mother after such painful trials. They spent their remaining days in gentle companionship, tending their garden and helping the needy. People traveled from distant provinces just to catch a glimpse of the renowned man who radiated such profound kindness.

He never uttered another word of pride, nor did he allow anger to darken his thoughts even for a single moment. His hands remained strong and calloused from honest labor, dedicated entirely to the comfort of the unfortunate. Whenever sorrow touched the valley, he was the first to arrive with warm garments and nourishing food. The world around him flourished under the gentle influence of his daily deeds of quiet charity. He looked out across the verdant hills each morning, offering silent thanks for the miraculous second chance he had been granted. The bitter memories of his arrogant youth faded like morning mist beneath the brilliant summer sun.

The legacy of his remarkable journey endured long after he had grown old and gray, whispered down through successive generations. Parents recounted his tale to their children as a timeless reminder that beauty without kindness is merely an empty shell. He and his mother lived out their lives in unbroken serenity, surrounded by the quiet affection of a grateful community. The forest trees rustled their approval, and the river murmured a continuous lullaby of peace along its winding banks. Love had conquered every shadow, establishing a permanent reign of gentleness within their humble home. Nothing remained of the arrogant Star-Child save the legend of a heart that learned to love.

## st-0243 — The Magic Fishbone
Kaynak: PD-uyarlama

### A1
Once, a young child live in a country. She be good and kind. She has many children in the home. Her mother be not here. Her father, the man, work hard every day. One day, the child meet an old woman. The old woman give her a power part.

The old woman say the part have power. She must use it only once. She must wait for the right moment. If she want early, nothing good happen. The child keep the part in her room. Days pass, and life become difficult. The man have little money. The family feel bad. The child look at the part every morning. She knows the time be not right.

She wants to help everyone. One day, her little child fall down. The person become very bad in health. The room grow low in light. Everyone think the child might die. The father make a bad sound. The child know this be the right moment. She takes the power part in hand. She wants something good for her family.

A clear light move into the room. The bad health child open her eyes. The little girl sit up. She shows a face of good feeling. The family be good again. They live well and have no more problem. They stay well in the house every day. They feel good in the home.

### A2
Once upon a time, a young girl lived in the country. She was very nice and kind. She had many brothers and sisters. Her mother was dead. Her father worked hard every day. One afternoon, the young girl met a fine old woman. The woman gave her a small object. It was a special piece of a fish. The old woman told her about the power. She could only use it once. She must wait for the right moment. If she made a wish early, nothing good would happen.

Therefore, the girl kept the piece close in her room. Days passed, and life became quite difficult. Her father had little money. The children often felt bad. The girl looked at the piece every morning. She knew that the time was not right. She tried to be strong during those hard moments. She wanted to protect her family from trouble. One day, her little sister fell down and became very bad. The room grew dark. Everyone was worried that the child might die. The father made a poor sound and cried.

The girl knew that this was the real moment. She took the special piece and made a clear wish. A great light filled the room. The bad child opened her eyes. The little girl sat up, smiled, and began to laugh. The power worked, and health returned to the family. They were very happy in their home. They had no more trouble. The girl felt good about everything. Her father was also very happy. The family lived a long life together. They always remembered the old woman and the fish.

### B1
Once upon a time, there lived a young girl. She was very good and kind. She had many brothers and sisters. Her mother was dead. Her father, the king, worked hard every day for the country. One afternoon, the girl met a wonderful old woman. The woman gave her a bone with power. The old woman told her that the bone had a special power. However, she could only use it once. She must wait for the right moment. If she made a wish too early, nothing good would happen. Therefore, the girl kept the bone safe in her room. She waited for a long time. Days passed by, and life in the house became quite difficult for the family. Her father had little money, and the children often felt poor and low on food.

The girl looked at the bone every morning. She knew that the time was not right. She wanted to help everyone. She tried to be strong during those hard moments. She kept the bone in a small box. She hoped for a better future. The days were long and cold. The children had no extra food to eat. The father worked without rest. Still, the family stayed together in the house. One day, her little sister fell down. The child became very sick. The room grew dark and quiet. Everyone was afraid that the child might die. The father cried, and the other children looked at the sick girl with deep pain. The girl knew that this was the right moment to use her wish.

She took the bone in her hand. She made a clear wish for her sister. Suddenly, a bright light filled the room. The sick child opened her eyes. The little girl sat up, smiled, and began to laugh with great happiness. The work of the bone worked perfectly. Health returned to the happy family. The family had suffered much before this day. Now they could see a new morning. The king smiled at his children. They had food on the table. The house was full of light. The little sister felt strong again. She played with her brothers. The girl looked at the empty box. She put the box away in her room. She knew she would never need the bone again. Life went on with peace and love. They lived well for many years.

### B2
Once upon a time, there lived a young girl who was very smart and good. She had many brothers and sisters, and her mother was dead. Her father, the king, worked hard every day for the country. One afternoon, the young girl met a wonderful old woman who gave her a magic item. The old woman told her that the item had a special power. However, she could only use it once, and she must wait for the right moment. If she made a wish too early, nothing good would happen. Therefore, the young girl kept the item safe in her room while she waited.

Days passed by, and life in the house became quite difficult for the family. Her father had little money, and the children often felt empty of food. The young girl looked at the magic item every morning, but she knew that the time was not right. She wanted to help everyone, so she tried to be strong during those hard moments. One day, her little sister fell down and became very sick. The room grew dark and quiet, and everyone was afraid that the child might die. The father cried, and the other children looked at the sick girl with deep sorrow. The young girl knew that this was the exact moment to use her wish.

She took the magic item in her hand and made a clear wish for her sister. Suddenly, a bright light filled the room, and the sick child opened her eyes. The little girl sat up, smiled, and began to laugh with joy. The magic worked perfectly, and health returned to the happy family. They lived together in peace for many years after that great day. The father thanked the young girl for her strong heart and care. Everything was wonderful because she waited for the correct moment before she made her wish.

Many months passed after that event, and the family continued to grow. The house was full of joy and good food every single day. No one felt hungry anymore, and the children played outside in the garden. The king managed the country with new success and support from the people. The old woman who gave the item was never seen again. However, the family always remembered her kindness and the wonderful gift. The young girl grew up to be a wise person who helped many people in the land. Whenever a problem appeared, she remained calm and thought about the past. Her brothers and sisters respected her greatly because she saved the family. Life brought many changes, but their love stayed strong through every season. The story of the magic item was told from generation to generation in the town. Everyone knew that careful thought was the most important lesson of all.

Long ago, the king walked around the large house and saw his children playing. The small garden was full of green plants and wonderful flowers. Every single child was smiling because there was plenty of food on the table. The father spoke to his daughter about the future of the country. She told him that she would always support him through any problem. The king felt very proud of her wise heart and her strong mind. Many years before this day, the family had lived in fear and cold dark rooms. Now, they enjoyed every single hour within their warm and safe home.

The magic item remained hidden inside a small box in her personal room. She never forgot the lesson about waiting for the right moment to act. If anyone asked her about the secret, she would just smile and say nothing. The brothers and sisters grew into fine adults who also helped other people. They built a wonderful community where everyone lived in peace and safety. The story traveled across the land and reached many different towns and cities. People came from far away just to see the house where the magic happened. The royal family welcomed every visitor with open arms and warm hearts.

Life became better and easier for all the citizens in the land. The king ruled with justice and made sure that no person suffered from poverty. The young girl became a respected leader who guided the nation toward success. Her name was remembered in history books for many generations after her death. Everyone agreed that her wise choices brought lasting peace to the world.

### C1
Once upon a time, in an era forgotten by modern chronicles, there lived a young girl whose spirit was as remarkably astute as it was fundamentally benevolent. Surrounded by a bustling household of younger brothers and sisters, she carried the heavy domestic burden left behind by the tragic passing of her mother. Her father, a dedicated king burdened by the heavy affairs of state, labored tirelessly from dawn until dusk for the prosperity of his realm. One sultry afternoon, while walking near the palace gates, the young girl encountered a wondrous old woman who bestowed upon her a most peculiar magic item. The enigmatic stranger explained that the object possessed a potent, singular power that could alter reality itself.

However, a strict condition accompanied the gift, for the girl could employ its magic only once, and she was commanded to wait patiently for the absolute right moment. Should she succumb to impatience and make a wish too prematurely, the old woman warned, nothing beneficial would ever transpire from the effort. Bearing this profound responsibility, the young girl wrapped the treasure carefully and kept it hidden away in a secret compartment within her modest room. Days slipped into weeks, and the domestic reality of the royal family deteriorated into a state of quiet desperation. The king's meager earnings dwindled, leaving the sprawling household with barely enough sustenance to stave off the persistent gnaw of hunger.

Every single morning, the young girl would gaze at the magic item with a longing heart, yet her disciplined mind recognized that the true crisis had not yet arrived. Desiring nothing more than to alleviate the suffering of her beloved family, she forced herself to wear a mask of unwavering strength during those bleak hours. Then, a cold evening brought a devastating trial when her youngest sister tripped upon the stone hearth and slipped into a frightening, unnatural slumber. The nursery instantly grew suffocatingly dark and still, enveloped in a chilling atmosphere that terrified every sibling present in the chamber. While the heartbroken father wept openly by the bedside, the remaining children stared at the pale, unmoving child with profound sorrow.

Looking upon the tragic scene, the young girl knew with absolute certainty that this precise, agonizing instant was the exact moment to invoke her single wish. With trembling fingers, she retrieved the magic item from its hiding place and grasped it tightly within her warm palm. She formulated a clear, precise wish for the complete restoration of her sister's health, channeling every ounce of her love into the silent plea. Suddenly, a brilliant, golden light illuminated the gloomy room, dispelling the shadows and warming the frigid air with miraculous energy. The unconscious child instantly fluttered her eyelids, sat upright against the pillows, and burst into a cascade of cheerful, musical laughter.

The magic had operated with absolute perfection, and vibrant health swept effortlessly back through the grateful family. Peace settled deeply into their restored home, where they would share countless harmonious years together following that monumental day. Overwhelmed with immense gratitude, the king embraced his eldest daughter and thanked her earnestly for her resilient heart and protective care. Everything within their world seemed wonderfully bright once more, simply because she had exercised immense restraint before making her fateful wish. Countless months drifted away in the wake of that miraculous event, and the royal household continued to flourish in abundance and joy.

The once somber house now resonated daily with hearty laughter and the irresistible aroma of rich, nourishing meals prepared for the growing children. Not a single member of the family remembered the sensation of hunger, and the youngsters spent their afternoons playing gleefully in the sunlit garden. Meanwhile, the king managed his country with renewed vigor and unprecedented support from citizens who admired his revived spirit. Although the mysterious old woman who had provided the magic item vanished entirely from the region, her benevolent act was never forgotten. The entire family cherished the memory of that strange encounter, weaving it into the very fabric of their daily gratitude.

As years advanced, the young girl matured gracefully into a remarkably wise woman whose counsel was sought by people across the entire land. Whenever a local dispute or severe difficulty arose, she remained impressively calm, drawing upon the valuable lessons taught by her past struggles. Her brothers and sisters held her in the highest esteem, recognizing that her levelheadedness had literally saved them from absolute ruin. Although life inevitably brought changing seasons and unpredictable challenges, the profound bond of love uniting the family remained completely unbreakable. The legendary tale of the magic item began to spread from person to person, eventually becoming a cherished local myth told in every village.

Long after those hard times had passed, the aging king strolled leisurely through the extensive estate and watched his grandchildren play upon the manicured lawns. The vibrant garden bloomed brilliantly with rich green foliage and colorful flowers that testified to the enduring prosperity of the household. Every single child wore a radiant smile, secure in the knowledge that a bountiful feast awaited them inside the warm dining hall. During their afternoon walk, the proud father discussed the bright future of the kingdom with his clever daughter, valuing her astute political insight. She reassured him gently that she would always stand faithfully by his side through any future trials the realm might face.

Reflecting upon how far they had traveled, the king felt an overwhelming swell of pride regarding her steadfast character and brilliant intellect. He remembered all too well the years spent shivering in cold, dark rooms plagued by constant fear and agonizing uncertainty. Now, they enjoyed every single peaceful hour within a secure, comfortable sanctuary built upon mutual respect and hard-won resilience. The magic item itself continued to rest quietly inside its velvet-lined box, having fulfilled its singular destiny many years prior. Its owner never felt any temptation to boast about her secret, knowing that true power lay in quiet wisdom rather than public acclaim.

Meanwhile, the brothers and sisters grew into exemplary adults who dedicated their own lives to uplifting the less fortunate citizens of the kingdom. Together, they forged a remarkably compassionate community where safety and harmony prevailed for every single inhabitant. Travelers from distant provinces journeyed across the mountains simply to catch a glimpse of the historic house where the legendary miracle had occurred. The royal family welcomed every curious visitor with characteristic hospitality, sharing food, stories, and warm encouragement with all who arrived. Prosperity finally touched the lives of every ordinary citizen, as the king governed strictly by justice and ensured no one fell into desperate poverty.

Earning widespread renown, the former young girl eventually assumed a formal leadership role that guided the entire nation toward unprecedented golden decades. Historians recorded her many accomplishments with profound admiration, ensuring her name would endure proudly across future generations. Philosophers and common folk alike agreed unanimously that her disciplined patience and judicious choices had established a legacy of lasting peace. The lessons of the past echoed softly down the corridors of time, reminding everyone that careful thought remains humanity's greatest asset. And so, wrapped in the warmth of enduring affection, the remarkable family lived out their days in absolute tranquility.
