## st-0253 — The Talkative Tortoise
Kaynak: PD-uyarlama

### A1
Long ago, an old friend lives near water. He has two little friends. They have no home now. A bad time comes. The water goes away. The water is all gone. The little friends want to leave. They must go to a new place. The old friend feels very bad. He does not want to stay. He wants to go too. He cannot walk. He must stay behind.

The two friends help him. They make a plan. They bring a long wood piece. The old friend bites the wood. Each friend takes one side. They hold the wood. They rise up high. The old friend must never open his face part. People look up at them. The people speak to them. The old friend hears the people. He wants to answer them. He opens his face part. He lets go of the wood. He falls down. He strikes the hard land. People should keep still.

Many days pass by. The old friend is back home. He is okay now. He knows a big fact. He must keep his face part closed. His friends look at him. They are very happy. Life goes on day by day. The friends live in quiet. No more bad times come. They stay together.

The sun shines every day. The friends look at the sky. They talk about the old days. They remember the big trip. They are good friends. They help each other. They have no more trouble. The old friend is very wise now. He does not talk too much. He listens to his friends. They are happy at home. The world is big. They stay in their place.

### A2
Long ago, a talking animal lived near blue water. He lived with two friends. They spent much time together every day. A bad summer came to the land. The water went away completely. The friends wanted to leave for a new place. The animal felt very bad inside. He did not want to stay behind alone. The friends wanted to help him very much.

They made a good plan together. They brought a long piece of plant material. The animal would hold the plant with his head and face. Each friend would take one side in its face. They were going to fly up high. The friends told him to never open his face part. Soon, the three animals rose high into the air. People down on the ground looked up. They spoke in great surprise.

They wondered what thing was in the air above. The animal heard the comments. He felt a strong wish to answer the group. He forgot the notice completely. He opened his face part to talk to the people. He let go of the plant. He began to fall down fast. The poor animal hit the hard ground. This story shows why people should keep still during trouble.

Many days passed after that event. The friends remembered the hard thing. They always tried to be ready. Life went on for them in the new place. They never forgot the bad summer. They knew that health was important. Every single day was a new present. They stayed together through every problem. The end of the story is clear now.

### B1
Long ago, a very happy animal lived near water with two bird friends. They spent much time together every day. Then, a bad summer came to the land. The water dried up completely because of the hot sun. The birds decided to leave that home for a new place. If they stay, they will find no water. The animal felt very sad because he did not want to stay behind alone. He has lived by this water for many years. Now, the summer has changed everything around them. The bird friends wanted to help him, so they made a plan. They brought a long stick. The animal would hold the stick with his mouth. Each bird would take one side of the stick in its face to fly up high. If the animal holds the stick well, the trip will be safe. The animal was warned that he must never open his mouth during the trip. He promised to keep quiet. Soon, the three friends rose high into the sky above the green land. People down on the ground looked up at them with great surprise and spoke. They wondered what strange thing was being carried by the birds in the air. The animal heard these comments and felt a strong urge to answer the crowd. He has never seen so many people in one place before. If he speaks now, he will fall down. He forgot the warning completely and opened his mouth to talk to the people. As soon as his mouth let go of the stick, he began to fall down fast. The poor animal hit the hard ground because he could not control his speech. If he had kept his mouth closed, he would have reached the new home safely. This simple story shows why people should always keep quiet when danger is near. The animal has lived many years near the water. The birds have known him for a long time. They have always tried to be good friends. When the summer came, everything became dry. The animal has lost his home now. The birds have made a clever plan. They have found a good stick for the journey. The animal has bitten the middle part of the stick. Each bird has taken one end in its face. They have flown high into the sky. Many people have looked up at them. The animal has heard the noise below. He has forgotten the warning from his friends. He has opened his mouth to speak. He has fallen down to the ground. If he stays quiet, he will not fall. If the summer had not been so hot, they would have stayed.

### B2
Long ago, a very loud animal lived near a quiet blue water place with two bird friends. They spent much time together, but a bad summer came and the water dried up. The birds decided to leave that home for a new place where water could be found. The animal felt very sad because he did not want to stay behind alone. The bird friends wanted to help him, so they made a brilliant plan. They brought a long stick which the animal would bite with his mouth. Each bird would take one side of the stick in its front mouth part to fly up high. The animal was warned that he must never open his mouth during the long trip. Soon, the three animals rose high into the sky above the green land. People down on the ground looked up at them with great surprise and spoke loudly. They wondered what strange thing was being carried by the birds in the air. The animal heard these comments and felt a strong urge to answer the crowd. He forgot the warning completely and opened his mouth to speak to the people. As soon as his mouth let go of the stick, he began to fall down fast. The poor creature hit the hard ground because he could not control his speech. This simple story shows why people should always keep quiet when danger is near. Many days passed after that event, and the friends often remembered the adventure. They lived in different parts of the world, but the memory stayed fresh in their minds. People everywhere would tell this story to their children during the evening hours. It became a lesson which was shared across many generations in the country. If anyone asks why silence is important, this account can be provided as proof. We must always remember that speaking at the wrong time can bring great trouble. Even though situations change, human nature remains very similar throughout history. Therefore, wise individuals prefer to listen carefully before they express any opinion. The world contains many dangers which cannot be avoided without proper care. By keeping our mouths shut during critical moments, we ensure our own safety. That old lesson remains true today just as it was true in ancient times. Every person should keep this in mind whenever a dangerous situation develops. Life offers many choices, and making the right choice determines our future success. Let us always value peace and quiet over simple words and empty noise. The journey of life requires both heart and thought to travel successfully. May we all learn from the poor creature who fell because of his speech.

Long ago in that same region, people would gather around the old fire to discuss the event. They knew that the creature had failed because he could not control his mouth. If the animal had kept his mouth shut, he would have reached the new home safely. Instead, he let go of the stick and fell down to the ground below. Every child in the village listened carefully as the older people told the story again and again. The parents taught their kids that silence is often better than speech. When danger is present, people must stay calm and quiet. If someone speaks too much, trouble will follow very soon. The birds tried to save their friend, but his own actions caused the disaster. Such events remind us that life requires great care and attention. We should always learn from the mistakes of others. If we remember this lesson, we will avoid many serious problems. The world is full of changes, and we must adapt to every new situation. Wise people know when to speak and when to remain silent. They do not waste their breath on empty noise or silly arguments. Instead, they listen to others and think deeply before they say anything. This habit helps them live quiet lives in a difficult world. The story of the birds and the animal remains famous across the entire country. Generations pass, but the truth within the tale stays the same. Anyone who hears the story will understand the power of silence. Let us keep this lesson in our minds as we face the future. Whenever we feel the urge to speak without thought, we should pause. A quiet mind is a safe mind, and peace is the best reward. May everyone find success and happiness by following this ancient advice.

### C1
Long ago, in a region scorched by an unforgiving summer, a remarkably loquacious tortoise made his dwelling near a diminishing pool of blue water. He shared this fragile sanctuary with two devoted avian companions whose plumage mirrored the morning sky. For seasons beyond counting, the trio had thrived in harmonious proximity, exchanging quiet confidences as the world turned peacefully around them. However, the arrival of a cruel and relentless drought threatened to strip their habitat of every remaining drop of moisture. The crystalline expanse shrank day by agonizing day until nothing lingered but cracked mud and weeping reeds.

Realizing the impending peril, the birds resolved to abandon their ancestral home for a distant paradise where water still flowed in abundance. A profound melancholy gripped the tortoise as he contemplated the grim prospect of being left behind in solitude. His short legs and heavy shell rendered him utterly incapable of crossing the vast, arid plains that stretched toward the horizon. Moved by compassion, his feathered friends devised an ingenious strategy to transport him across the perilous skies. They procured a sturdy, resinous branch of considerable length, instructing the tortoise to clamp his powerful beak firmly around its center.

The mechanics of the rescue mission were as delicate as they were daring. Each bird took hold of one extremity of the wooden rod with their strong beaks, preparing to lift their passenger into the upper air. Before taking flight, they issued a strict and solemn admonition regarding the absolute necessity of silence. Not a single sound could escape the tortoise's throat, lest he loosen his grip and plunge to certain doom. Nodding resolutely, the creature signaled his complete understanding of the terms upon which his survival depended.

With a coordinated surge of muscle and wing, the three companions rose gracefully above the desiccated landscape. The emerald valleys and winding rivers of their youth drifted downward, transforming into a patchwork quilt of green and brown. As they soared at breathtaking altitudes, villagers working in the sunlit fields below paused to shade their eyes against the glare. Astonished peasants pointed upward, shouting in absolute disbelief at the surreal spectacle unfolding in the heavens. They craned their necks to observe a pair of birds bearing a heavily armored reptile through the clouds on a humble stick.

The sudden clamor of the crowd below rippled through the quiet air, striking the ears of the soaring tortoise. He felt a surging wave of indignation and vanity as he listened to the confused speculations of the ignorant farmers. How could they be so utterly misguided about his noble participation in this magnificent flight? The temptation to correct their absurd assumptions grew into an overwhelming, intoxicating urge within his chest. He momentarily forgot the grave warnings of his companions, succumbing entirely to the irresistible desire to voice his opinion.

Opening his mouth to hurl a biting retort down at the gaping spectators, the tortoise committed a fatal error. The moment his jaws parted, the wooden rod slipped effortlessly from his grip. Suspended for a single heart-stopping fraction of a second in the empty air, he realized the terrifying magnitude of his mistake. Gravity reclaimed him with ruthless efficiency, pulling his heavy shell downward in a rapid, uncontrolled descent. The wind rushed fiercely past his ears as the earth rushed upward to meet him with unyielding hardness.

The poor creature struck the unforgiving ground with devastating force, paying the ultimate price for his inability to govern his tongue. Silence, once a golden shield protecting him from danger, now blanketed the scene of his tragic downfall. In the days that followed, the grieving birds mourned their friend while completing their arduous journey to the distant water. They settled in a verdant new land, yet the memory of their companion remained as fresh as the morning dew. The tale of the talkative tortoise traveled far and wide, whispered from parent to child across countless generations.

Long afterward, villagers would gather around glowing hearths during the cold evening hours to recount the famous event. They analyzed every detail of the disaster, reminding the young ones that discipline is the foundation of safety. If the animal had merely kept his mouth shut against the provocation of the crowd, he would have reached paradise. Instead, his vanity proved far heavier than his shell, dragging him down from the sky through sheer lack of restraint. Parents taught their children that speaking without forethought is a perilous luxury no wise person can afford.

When danger looms on the horizon, agitation and loud protests serve no purpose other than inviting further disaster. Those who survive turbulent times are invariably those who maintain their composure and observe the world in stillness. The birds had done everything in their power to save their friend, but no rescue can succeed against self-destruction. Such profound lessons remind humanity that life demands constant vigilance, patience, and careful adaptation. Wise individuals understand intuitively when speech is appropriate and when absolute quiet is the only correct course.

They refuse to waste their precious breath on empty arguments or trivial vanities that yield nothing but trouble. Instead, they listen intently to the wisdom of others, pondering their words deeply before venturing an opinion. This quiet habit shields them from the hidden traps scattered across an unpredictable and challenging world. The ancient story of the flying tortoise stands as an enduring monument to the power of discretion. Generations pass into history, but the core truth of the fable remains as unshakeable as the earth itself.

Whenever an impulse to speak rashly threatens to overcome the mind, one should pause and remember the falling traveler. A disciplined mind is the safest sanctuary against the storms that constantly sweep across human existence. Peace and quiet are invariably more valuable than any clever remark or boastful declaration. The journey of life requires both courage and profound mental restraint to navigate successfully to the end. May all who hear this ancient account learn the true value of silence before they face their own moments of trial.

## st-0254 — The Brahmin and the Pots (Castle in Air)

### A1
There live a man. He has no much money. He works hard every day. He keeps every day. He has enough money now. He buys several large things. He buys many large boxes. The boxes hold good food. One evening, he sit down. He stays in his room. He thinks about future life. He wants to sell food. He will get much money. He will get a big house. He wants a woman and family. He wants other people to work. He thinks about his great life.

In a mind view, his woman move slow. He feels very bad. He feels very strong feeling. He moves up his lower body part. He goes into open place. He not think about boxes. ### B2
There once lived a poor man who worked hard every day to earn a little money. He saved every single coin until he had enough to buy several pots filled with sweet food. He kept these valuable pots in his small room, placing them carefully where he could see them all the time. Day after day, he looked at his pots and thought about how his life would change. He wanted to become very rich, and he believed that his hard work would eventually bring him success. He always worked from early morning until late night, hoping for a better future. Many people in the town knew him as a quiet person who rarely spoke to anyone. However, his mind was constantly filled with grand ideas about wealth and luxury. He imagined that one day he would possess large amounts of gold and silver. Such thoughts gave him energy when he felt tired from his daily labor. He believed that money could solve every problem which troubled him. Without money, life seemed difficult and bad to him. Therefore, he guarded his funds with great care and never spent a single coin on extra things. Every time he added a new coin to his box, his heart felt full of joy. He pictured the day when he would finally stop working and live like a king. His small room felt very poor, but his dreams were rich and grand. He often smiled while looking at the pots that contained his food. Those pots represented the first step toward his primary goal of becoming a wealthy man. He was sure that nothing could stop him from achieving success if he continued to save. Every single moment of his life was dedicated to this single purpose.

One evening, as he lay on his bed, he began to dream about his future wealth. If he sold all his food, he would make a lot of money. With that money, he could buy more pots and become even richer. He thought about how rich men lived in big houses with many servants. He imagined that a rich life would bring him a wonderful wife and a fine family. In his dream, he walked through large rooms filled with beautiful furniture and expensive art. He saw himself wearing fine clothes and eating good meals every single day. People would stand before him and show him great respect wherever he went. His business would grow larger, and his name would become famous across the entire country. He would own many horses and transport that would take him to wonderful places. Every person in the city would know how successful he had become. He felt a big sense of pride as he pictured his future. Nothing could possibly go wrong in this perfect world which he had built in his mind. He felt that he truly deserved all this happiness because of his past suffering. The vision became so clear that he could almost hear the voices of his staff. He smiled broadly in the dark room, completely lost in his wonderful story. The hours passed quickly while he enjoyed this pleasant state of mind. He believed that his dreams were already turning into reality. His heart beat fast with excitement as he imagined the wedding day with his future wife. Everything seemed so bright and promising in the quiet evening hours.

He pictured himself resting while other people worked for him. In his dream, his wife became lazy, and he grew very angry with her. To show his anger, he raised his leg and kicked out hard into the empty air. He forgot that the pots were hanging right above him. His foot hit the pots with a loud noise, and every single one fell down to the floor and broke into pieces. The sudden sound broke the deep silence of the small room. The pots which he had saved for so long were now completely destroyed. He understood that dreaming about things which had not happened yet was not helpful. He finally decided that he should focus on reality rather than on silly ideas. Tears came to his eyes as he looked at the mess on the floor. All his hard-earned money was gone in less than a second. The big house, the servants, and the wonderful family disappeared like smoke. He was still just a poor man sitting in a small, empty room. The lesson was hard, but he knew he had to accept the truth. From that day forward, he tried to live in the present moment instead of chasing impossible dreams.

### C1
In a quiet corner of the ancient town, a humble man labored from dawn until dusk just to earn a meager wage. He possessed a silent, solitary nature, rarely exchanging words with his neighbors who walked the dusty streets. While others sought comfort in companionship, his mind remained entirely consumed by grand ambitions of wealth and luxury. Every single coin he earned through his relentless toil was carefully saved inside a secure wooden box. He believed that money alone possessed the power to dissolve every hardship that weighed upon his spirit. Without financial security, life appeared bleak, burdensome, and thoroughly unbearable to his anxious eyes. Day after day, he guarded his growing funds with meticulous care, denying himself even the smallest of simple pleasures.

His modest living quarters remained stark and impoverished, yet his internal world swelled with extravagant visions. He eventually accumulated enough surplus to purchase several large earthenware pots filled with sweet, preserved provisions. These valuable containers were placed carefully in his small room, positioned precisely where his eyes could linger upon them constantly. Whenever fatigue threatened to overwhelm his limbs during long hours of labor, he glanced at the pots for renewed energy. They represented the foundational first step toward his ultimate objective of escaping poverty forever. He was entirely convinced that unwavering persistence would inevitably bring him the opulent future he craved. Not a single moment of his waking life passed without some calculation regarding his financial ascent.

Each time he dropped a newly earned coin into his hidden box, a profound surge of joy flooded his chest. He frequently smiled in the dim light, envisioning the magnificent day when he would finally abandon his exhausting labor. His imagination painted vivid portraits of a life lived in absolute ease, mirroring the habits of kings and nobility. The cramped, dreary room seemed to vanish whenever he allowed himself to dwell upon these intoxicating daydreams of grandeur. He felt a deep, unshakable certainty that nothing could possibly obstruct his path toward total financial triumph. To him, the pots were not merely containers of food, but magical seeds from which a golden empire would sprout. He treasured them with a reverence usually reserved for sacred relics or priceless heirlooms.

One sultry evening, as twilight surrendered to a heavy darkness, he reclined upon his narrow cot to rest. His tired muscles relaxed, yet his hyperactive mind immediately resumed its familiar journey into the realm of fantasy. If he were to sell his accumulated sweet provisions at the market, a substantial sum of money would instantly materialize. With those vital funds, he could purchase even more pots of goods, multiplying his inventory exponentially. The economic cycle he devised in his thoughts promised endless expansion and staggering profits within a remarkably short span of time. He calculated the exact profit margins with the precision of a seasoned merchant overseeing a vast commercial enterprise. Every numerical detail aligned perfectly in his mind, reinforcing his absolute confidence in the impending scheme.

As the night deepened, his ambitious reverie escalated far beyond simple commerce into matters of domestic status. He imagined himself residing in a sprawling mansion surrounded by manicured gardens and whispering fountains. A retinue of obedient servants would scurry to fulfill his every whim at the mere snap of his fingers. Naturally, such immense wealth would attract a wonderful wife of noble bearing and a fine family to secure his legacy. He pictured himself strolling through vaulted halls adorned with magnificent furnishings and expensive artistic masterpieces. Everywhere he traveled, people would step aside, lowering their heads in deep respect to acknowledge his towering influence. His name would soon become legendary across the entire country as a paragon of unmatched success.

The sheer scale of his imagined opulence filled him with an intoxicating sense of pride and self-importance. He envisioned stables filled with magnificent horses and private carriages ready to transport him to exotic destinations. Not a single person in the entire city would remain unaware of his extraordinary rise from absolute obscurity. He truly believed he deserved every ounce of this luxurious future as compensation for his years of suffering. The psychological warmth of the dream banished the chill of the dark, lonely room entirely from his consciousness. So vivid did the illusion become that he fancied he could actually hear the murmurs of his imaginary staff. A broad smile stretched across his face while he luxuriated in the delightful fiction of his own making.

Hours slipped away silently as he drifted deeper into the elaborate narrative unfolding inside his fertile imagination. In this advanced stage of the dream, he pictured himself resting lazily while others labored tirelessly on his behalf. Within this constructed domestic scene, his idealized wife suddenly revealed a stubborn and lazy disposition. The imagined insolence grated against his newly acquired sense of aristocratic dignity and stirred a sudden, fierce anger. He envisioned himself shouting corrections at her, demanding immediate obedience and punishing her perceived idleness with harsh words. His pulse raced with indignation as the argument within his head escalated to a dramatic climax. The frustration felt utterly authentic, though it was born entirely from a phantom disagreement in an empty room.

Desperate to demonstrate the absolute authority he held within his imaginary household, he decided to take decisive action. To emphasize his burning anger in the dream, he raised his leg and kicked out violently into the dark air. He completely forgot the fragile reality of his physical surroundings and the precarious position of his precious investments. His foot struck the suspended earthenware pots with a sudden, devastating force that shattered the midnight silence. A sharp crack echoed through the cramped space as the heavy containers were dislodged from their wooden supports. Gravity took immediate control, sending every single pot tumbling downward toward the hard wooden floor below. They smashed into a chaotic thousand pieces upon impact, releasing their sticky, sweet contents everywhere.

The violent cacophony shocked his nervous system, dragging him abruptly from his elaborate slumber back to harsh reality. He sat bolt upright, his eyes flying open to confront the suffocating darkness of his actual room. The thick, sugary provisions coated the floorboards in a chaotic mess, spelling absolute ruin for his grand ambitions. He stared blankly into the shadows, slowly comprehending the catastrophic magnitude of his own careless physical movement. The pots he had painstakingly acquired and guarded for so long were now completely and irreparably destroyed. A bitter wave of realization washed over him as he surveyed the sticky devastation surrounding his bed. All the fruits of his relentless daily labor had vanished in less than a single, foolish second.

Tears of profound frustration welled in his eyes as he assessed the total loss of his savings and dreams. There was no escaping the painful truth that his own impulsive kick had caused this entirely avoidable disaster. He had no one else in the world to blame for the catastrophe that now crippled his future. The sprawling mansion, the obedient servants, and the magnificent family dissolved instantly like wisps of smoke in the wind. He remained simply a poor, exhausted man sitting alone in a small, dark, and empty room. The hard lesson settled heavily upon his shoulders, demanding immediate acceptance of his actual, unvarnished circumstances. His foolish daydreaming had blinded him to the tangible reality he should have been protecting all along.

From that sobering night onward, the chastened man vowed to anchor his mind firmly in the present moment. He understood that chasing impossible futures while neglecting reality was a dangerous path to self-destruction. The destruction of his pots served as an eternal reminder of the fragility inherent in idle speculation. Although his wealth was gone and his room was empty, a new wisdom began to take root within him. He returned to his daily labor the very next morning with a quieter, more grounded perspective on existence. Instead of dwelling on kingdoms built of air, he focused his energy on the honest work beneath his hands. True security, he finally learned, could only be constructed through patient action rather than careless dreams.

## st-0255 — The Crane and the Crab

### A1

The little person holds the bird hard. The bird cannot move. They reach the land. The friend does not let go. The bird is bad. The bird gets a bad day. The good friend lives well.

Fish swam under the surface while the bird watched them. The bird grew old and could not run after food now. That problem made the bird create a very bad plan. The animal came to the water and saw the sad bird.

### B2
Once upon a time, a big bird lived near a clear water pool. The animal loved to eat fish every single day of its long life. However, growing old made catching food quite difficult. Because running fast was no longer possible, the smart animal needed a good plan. The bird stood by the water and looked very sad. Soon, a little animal came close and asked why the big bird was crying. The bird told a story about danger coming to the pool. The animal said that men would soon come to take all the fish away. The creature, which felt very worried, asked if there was any safe place. The bird pointed toward a lovely lake far over the hills and offered help.

Day after day, the bird carried fish away in its long mouth. Instead of helping them, the animal ate every single one. Soon, it was the turn of the creature to take the trip. The animal climbed up, and they flew high above the land. Looking down, the smart creature saw many fish bones on the ground and realized the truth. Without any fear, the small creature used its strong legs to hold the bird tight. The bird could not move or breathe properly until it promised to bring the animal safely back down.

When they reached the ground again, the quick animal did not let go. The bad bird was finally hurt for being so bad. From that day, the smart animal lived happily and safely near the water. Every single fish that remained in the pool felt safe because the bad bird was gone forever. The community of creatures celebrated their freedom under the bright sun and lived in peace for many years. They always remembered the great danger which had threatened their home. If any new problem ever appeared, they would work together to solve it without fear. Life by the clear pool became wonderful, and every day brought new joy to the happy animals.

### C1
Beside the translucent expanse of a tranquil woodland pool, a solitary crane had spent long decades mastering the quiet art of the hunt. Every single day of its enduring life had been dedicated to capturing the shimmering fish that populated the shaded water. However, the relentless passing of seasons had finally claimed the bird's once formidable speed and agility. Age had rendered the daily pursuit of sustenance an exhausting and nearly impossible endeavor. Realizing that brute force could no longer secure its meals, the cunning predator recognized the necessity of a calculated deception.

Standing motionless at the water's edge, the crane adopted a posture of profound melancholy and despair. It pulled its long neck into its grey feathers and allowed a single, brilliant drop of moisture to cling to its beak. Soon enough, a little crab who lived among the reeds approached the imposing bird with hesitant caution. Moved by curiosity and growing dread, the small creature inquired why the grand crane appeared so utterly desolate. In response, the bird sighed heavily, projecting an air of manufactured tragedy and unbearable sorrow.

The crane wove a meticulous web of fabrication concerning an impending catastrophe that threatened the entire aquatic community. With trembling grace, the bird claimed to have overheard men discussing their plans to drain the pool entirely. These merciless humans, the crane insisted, would soon arrive with massive nets to capture every single fish and creature dwelling within the basin. The listening crab felt a cold wave of panic wash over its fragile shell as the horrifying scenario unfolded. Overwhelmed by distress, the small animal pleaded to know if any sanctuary existed far away from this doomed habitat.

Seizing the moment, the crane raised its long beak and pointed toward the distant, undulating line of green hills. It described a magnificent, hidden lake where the water remained forever cool and abundant with untouched life. With an air of supreme benevolence, the bird offered to transport the inhabitants one by one to this utopian refuge. Relief swept through the trusting crab, blinding it to the subtle glint of calculation in the bird's unblinking eyes. Never once did the innocent creature suspect that the promised sanctuary was merely a cruel illusion designed to feed a starving monster.

Day after day, the crane dutifully carried fish away in its long beak under the guise of relocation. Instead of guiding them toward any distant paradise, the deceitful animal merely flew to a jagged pile of grey boulders. There, untouched by pity, the bird consumed every single passenger before they could even comprehend the betrayal. The bones of the trusting victims accumulated in a grim heap beneath the rocks, whitening under the relentless sun. Oblivious to the slaughter, the remaining inhabitants of the pool waited eagerly for their turn to be saved.

Eventually, the appointed hour arrived for the little crab to take the fateful journey toward the promised lake. Climbing carefully upon the crane's broad back, the small animal settled in preparation for the long flight. The bird launched itself into the azure sky, carrying its passenger high above the familiar trees and winding rivers. Below them, the landscape stretched out in a vast, indifferent tapestry of green fields and distant blue shadows. As they soared over a desolate ridge of stone, the crab peered downward and noticed something entirely unexpected.

Scattered across the grey earth below lay the glistening white skeletons of countless fish from the pool. The terrifying truth crystallized in the crab's mind with the blinding clarity of a sudden lightning strike. There was no secret lake, and there was no salvation; the benevolent guide was nothing more than a ruthless executioner. A surge of righteous fury replaced the initial wave of shock within the small creature's heart. Without a moment of hesitation, the intelligent animal resolved that it would not become another nameless victim.

Gathering all its courage and physical strength, the crab clamped its powerful claws around the crane's slender neck. It tightened its grip with an iron determination, anchoring itself firmly against the rushing wind of the high altitude. The crane gasped in sudden terror as the sharp shell pressed cruelly against its vulnerable throat. Unable to breathe properly or maintain its steady flight, the massive bird began to spiral downward in a panic. Desperate for air, the choking creature twisted wildly through the sky, but the stubborn crab refused to loosen its hold.

They crashed heavily onto the very ground from which their deceitful journey had begun weeks before. Even as the dust settled around them, the quick-witted crab maintained its unyielding grip on the treacherous bird's neck. The wicked crane realized that its life hung entirely by a thread held in the claws of its intended victim. Squeezing tighter still, the small animal forced the trembling bird to utter a solemn promise of absolute surrender. Only after receiving a guarantee of safe conduct did the victorious crab finally release its fierce embrace.

Released from the near-fatal grasp, the chastened bird fled the region entirely, never to return to the tranquil pool. From that remarkable day forward, the clever crab lived safely and happily among the familiar reeds and cool water. Every single fish that remained in the basin could now swim without fear, knowing the terrible threat was gone forever. A profound sense of peace settled over the aquatic community, and the animals celebrated their hard-won freedom. Under the bright, warm sun, they gathered to rejoice in a newfound security that would endure for many seasons.

They never forgot the great danger that had once threatened to destroy their entire community and way of life. Should any new problem ever arise in the future, the creatures knew they would stand together to solve it. Fear no longer ruled their days, replaced instead by a quiet strength born from unity and shared experience. Life by the clear pool blossomed into something truly wonderful, filled with the simple joy of each passing afternoon. Every single morning brought new light and happiness to the animals who had learned the true value of vigilance.

## st-0256 — The Wise Little Girl
Kaynak: PD-uyarlama

### A1
Two men live in a place. One man has a lot of money. He is a bad person. The other man has no money. He is a good person. He gives a car to the head person. The head person likes the car. He gives money to the good man. The bad man is very bad. He says the car is his. The head person gives three hard questions. They must answer by the next day. The good man is very bad now. His little child is very good. She knows the answers to the questions. She tells her father not to care.

Next morning, the men see the head person. The good man gives the good answers. He says his little child helps him. The head person wants to see the child. She comes to the big house. The head person gives her one test. She must come with no car. She must come with a car. She must come with no step. She must come with a step.

She has an old room item. A car brings her on the way. The head person is very good with her. He let her father keep the money. The little child is very able. Her father is very fine now. They live in the big house. The bad man go away from the place. Every person is very good today. The story ends here with much love.

### A2
Long ago, two brothers lived in a small village. One brother had much money, but he always wanted more. The other brother had little, yet he was kind. Once, the poor brother gave an animal to the leader.

The leader liked the present very much. He asked the poor man about his land. The poor man explained that he had very little. Then the leader gave him a large amount of money. The brother with money heard about this. He became very unhappy and went to see the leader.

He said the animal was really his property. The leader gave them a hard test. He asked three difficult questions for the next day. They would face trouble if they failed the test. The poor brother went home with a heavy heart. He had a young little girl. She always gave him good help.

She listened to the questions and told him not to worry. She knew how to answer each problem. The next morning, both brothers stood before the leader. The wealthy man spoke badly. The poor brother gave the clever answers. The leader was surprised by these good answers. The good man told the true fact that his little girl helped him.

The leader wanted to see this good child. She came to the large house. The leader gave her one final test. She must come not wearing clothes and also not without clothes. She must come not walking and also not traveling. The clever girl understood the game.

She covered herself in an old fishing cover. This meant she was not wearing clothes and not without clothes. The clever child put an animal close to her feet. The animal pulled her along the road. She was not walking and not traveling. The leader was surprised and let her father keep the money.

### B1
Long ago, two brothers lived in a small village as poor farmers. One brother had much land and money, but he always wanted more. The other brother had almost nothing, yet he was kind and good. Once, the poor brother gave a lovely horse to the rich king. The king was very pleased and asked about his land. The poor man explained that he was a simple farmer. The king decided to give him a bag of gold money. The rich brother became extremely angry when he learned about this. The brother who wanted more went to the king and claimed the horse. The king gave them three hard questions to find the truth. They must solve these before the next day. If they failed, serious trouble would come to them. The poor brother went home with a heavy heart. He had a small girl who always gave good advice.

She listened carefully and told her father not to worry. The child knew how each problem could be solved. The next morning, both brothers stood before the king. The poor brother provided the answers from his daughter. The king was surprised by the words. The good man admitted that his little girl helped him. The king wanted to see this child at the big house. When she arrived, the king gave her one final challenge. She must come neither dressed nor without clothes. She must come neither walking nor riding. The girl prepared a very special plan. She covered herself in an old fishing net. Then she tied an animal to her feet to pull her. She reached the government doors in this strange way. The king was amazed and let her father keep the gold.

Many years have passed since that day in the village. The poor family has lived well because of the gold. If anyone asks about the clever girl, the people tell the story. They remember how she solved the hard questions for her father. The king has never forgotten the strange visit. He always speaks well of the smart child. The rich brother has learned to be less greedy. He has understood that money cannot buy true cleverness. Life in the village has changed for the better. The farmers work hard and help each other every day. Every person in the country knows this famous tale now. The little girl has grown into a wise young woman. She still gives great advice to everyone around her. Her father looks at her with pride and deep love. They have found peace and happiness in their small home.

### B2
Long ago, there lived two brothers who were poor farmers in a small village. One brother had much wealth, but he was very mean and shared nothing with anyone. The other brother had almost nothing, yet he was kind and always ready to help people. Once, the poor brother gave a lovely horse to the rich king as a gift. He wanted to show his respect because he was an honest man who valued peace. The king was very pleased with the gift and asked the poor man how much land he owned. The poor man explained that he was a simple farmer with very little property. Hearing this, the king decided to give him a bag of gold coins. When the rich brother learned about the gold, he became extremely angry and sad. He felt that the king should have given the money to him instead. The mean brother went quickly to the king and claimed that the horse was really his. The king, who wanted to know the truth, gave them a difficult test. He asked them three hard questions which must be solved before the next day. If they failed, they would face serious trouble from the royal court.

The poor brother went home with a heavy heart, feeling completely alone and weak. He had a smart little girl who always gave him good advice when problems appeared. She listened carefully to the king questions and told her father not to worry. The little girl knew exactly how each problem could be solved through smart thinking. She believed that they would find a way to pass the test successfully. The next morning, both brothers stood before the king to give their answers. The rich brother spoke badly, but the poor brother provided the smart answers which his daughter had taught him. The king was surprised and realized that the answers were too smart for the poor farmer. When the king asked who had helped him, the honest man admitted that his little girl had solved the questions. The king wanted to see this smart child and commanded that she be brought to the castle.

When the little girl arrived, the king gave her one final challenge to test her mind. He told her to come to the castle neither dressed nor not dressed, neither walking nor riding. The smart girl understood the king game and prepared a very special plan. She wrapped herself in an old fishing net so that she was neither fully dressed nor not dressed. Then she tied an animal to her feet and let the creature drag her along the road. In this way, she was neither walking nor riding when she finally reached the royal gates. The king was so amazed by her intelligence that he praised her greatly and let her father keep the gold. The poor brother lived happily for many years with his wonderful family. He always remembered how his smart daughter had saved them from big trouble.

People in the village talked about the smart child who solved the royal questions. No one ever forgot the day when the poor farmer received the gold coins. Life in the small village continued quietly while the family enjoyed their new wealth. The king often thought about the wise little girl and her strange journey to the castle. He knew that true intelligence could be found in the most surprising places. Both brothers learned important lessons about life, love, and truth. The rich brother finally understood that money could not buy everything. He tried to change his ways and started to help his neighbors when they needed assistance. The poor brother remained kind and always shared what little he had with others.

Their story became a famous tale which was told across the entire country for generations. Children listened with wide eyes while their parents repeated the wonderful story. Everybody agreed that the little girl was the smartest person in the whole country. The king made sure that peace and quiet ruled over the land for all time. And so the happy days went by without any further trouble for the honest farmer and his family.

### C1
In a secluded rural valley where time seemed suspended, two brothers toiled as humble farmers. Though bound by blood, their paths diverged sharply in spirit and fortune. The elder brother accumulated substantial wealth, yet his heart remained tightly shuttered against the needs of others. He hoarded his possessions, believing that charity was merely a foolish waste. Conversely, the younger brother possessed almost nothing of material value. Yet, his generous nature radiated warmth, and he was perpetually inclined to assist anyone in distress. Driven by an innate sense of reverence and a desire for peace, the impoverished farmer decided to make a remarkable gesture. He selected his finest possession, a magnificent horse, and led it across the hills as a tribute to the sovereign. Not once did he anticipate a reward; rather, he simply wished to honor the monarch with integrity. Touched by such profound sincerity, the king received the magnificent animal with deep appreciation. He inquired gently about the farmer's livelihood, wanting to understand the circumstances of such a generous giver. With quiet dignity, the farmer explained that his property consisted of little more than a patch of earth and a weathered cottage. Moved by the stark contrast between the man's poverty and his generous spirit, the king instructed his treasurer to bestow a heavy purse of gold upon him. It was a fortune that promised to transform the struggling farmer's daily existence. When news of this royal favor reached the ears of the elder brother, bitterness immediately poisoned his mind. He burned with jealousy, convinced that the monarch had committed a grave error by bypassing his own substantial estate. Consumed by greed, the wealthy brother hurried to the royal palace to challenge the transaction. He boldly claimed that the magnificent horse had actually belonged to his own stables, accusing the younger sibling of theft and deception. Intrigued by the conflicting claims and determined to uncover the truth, the king devised a severe test. He commanded both men to solve three intricate riddles before the arrival of the following dawn. Failure to produce the correct answers, he warned, would result in harsh punishment from the royal court.

Returning to his modest dwelling, the poor farmer carried a burden heavier than any stone he had ever lifted. He felt entirely unequipped for the intellectual combat demanded by the throne. Fortunately, he shared his roof with a remarkably perceptive daughter whose mind was remarkably sharp. Having observed her father's distress, she listened carefully as he recounted the royal challenge. Noticing his despair, she urged him to cast aside his worries and trust in her guidance. The clever girl possessed an intuitive grasp of human nature and logic, seeing straight through the riddles that had paralyzed her father. She assured him that they would outwit the king's tests through careful thought rather than brute force. Reassured by her steady confidence, the father regained a measure of hope as night fell over the valley. When morning arrived, the two brothers stood once again before the imposing gates of the royal palace. While the wealthy brother stumbled through clumsy and arrogant replies, the poor farmer recited the brilliant answers his daughter had carefully prepared. Astonishment rippled through the royal court as the king listened to the precise and elegant solutions. It was immediately apparent that such sophisticated reasoning far exceeded the simple farmer's usual capabilities. Pressured by the monarch to reveal the true source of his wisdom, the honest man confessed the truth without hesitation. He explained that his young daughter had unraveled the difficult questions. Intrigued by this revelation, the king demanded that the extraordinary child be brought immediately to the castle for a final demonstration of her intellect. Upon her arrival in the grand hall, the king decided to test the limits of her reputation with one final paradox. He commanded her to present herself before him neither dressed nor not dressed, and neither walking nor riding. A lesser mind would have faltered under the weight of such an impossible contradiction. However, the wise girl merely smiled, recognizing the playful nature of the royal challenge. She immediately set to work, determined to satisfy the monarch's exacting conditions with creative precision.

Returning briefly to the courtyard, she found an old, discarded fishing net and wrapped it securely around her frame. In this ingenious attire, she was neither fully clothed nor completely naked, satisfying the first half of the paradox. For the second requirement, she secured a strong hound to her ankles and permitted the creature to drag her slowly along the dirt road. Thus, she arrived at the royal gates neither walking on her own feet nor riding upon a mount. The king watched her approach from his balcony, utterly captivated by her brilliant solution. Overwhelmed with admiration, the monarch praised the young girl publicly and confirmed that her father should retain every coin of the gold. The impoverished family returned to their village, where they lived comfortably for many years, shielded from sudden hardship. The father never forgot how his daughter's remarkable mind had rescued them from disaster. Meanwhile, the village buzzed with endless conversations about the clever child who had conquered the royal riddles. The king himself often pondered her ingenuity, realizing that true wisdom frequently resides in the most unexpected corners of society. Gradually, the transformative experience softened the harsh edges of the wealthy brother's character. Witnessing the enduring happiness of his sibling, he finally comprehended that wealth alone could never purchase genuine respect or peace. He began to alter his conduct, offering assistance to struggling neighbors instead of turning them away. The kind brother continued his generous habits, sharing his newfound comfort with those in genuine need. Thus, the entire community settled into a quiet harmony, sustained by the lessons learned during that unforgettable test.

Generations later, villagers still gathered around hearths to recount the tale of the wise little girl. Children listened with wide eyes, absorbing the moral that intellect and kindness triumph over greed. The monarch ensured that peace prevailed throughout his domain, protecting his subjects from needless conflict. The memory of the humble farmer and his brilliant daughter endured as a symbol of hope. And so the peaceful days flowed onward, undisturbed by further trials for the grateful family.

## st-0257 — Snow Maiden (Snegurochka)
Kaynak: PD-uyarlama

### A1
An old man lives in a house. An old woman lives with him. They have no child here. They feel very bad. They look outside the door. They want a little child. The man talk about a plan. They go out of the house. They build a child with things. They make eyes and other parts. The child become a girl. They call her Snegurochka. They love the girl much. They give her a good home.

The girl grow up very much. Days pass by every week. She looks very bad each day. Then a change come over the land. Snegurochka feel very bad. Other young people go to the place. They ask Snegurochka to go with them. Her mother tell her to go. The group walk through the land. They play many games together. Children go over the water. Snegurochka step near the big light. She goes into water right then. Nothing stay after that time. Only a little light water remain.

The old man and woman look. They see only water. They feel very bad again. They remember the little girl. They stay in the house. Days pass by without the child. The old people live on there. They think about her all day. They love her still. They feel for her every day. Life goes on for them. They keep her name in mind. They sit by the door often. They look out at the place. They hope to see her back.

### A2
Once upon a time, an old man and woman lived together. They had no child. This made them feel very low. One morning, the white cold stuff fell across the ground. The couple looked outside through the window. The man suggested they should build a lovely child. They went outside into the garden. They began to shape the cold water and stuff. They gave her two blue eyes and nice hair. Suddenly, the girl came to life with a full sound. The happy parents named her Snegurochka. They loved her very much every day. She grew up quickly and became very beautiful. When the new season arrived, Snegurochka started to look low and low. Her parents watched her with much care. Summer came soon after with high hot weather. Other young people called Snegurochka to the forest. Her mother told her that going outside would be good. The group walked through the trees, singing songs. The children built a small fire. They started to move over the hot fire. Snegurochka stepped near the fire. She suddenly turned into a little water. Nothing was left except a light view. Her parents felt very low about this loss. They remembered her nice face every day. The little girl lived in their hearts all time. They always talked about the lovely white child. Seasons would change again and again. Life went on for the old couple in the house.

The old couple remembered the little girl. They stayed in the house together. Days passed by very slowly for them. They walked in the garden every morning. They looked at the green trees and flowers. Sometimes they talked about the winter time. The white cold stuff would return again. They always kept her memory in mind. Life continued for the old man and woman. They found peace in their quiet home. Every evening they sat near the table. They ate food and spoke softly. The house was old but very comfortable. They never forgot the lovely child. Years passed by in the small village. The old couple remained together until the end. Their story stayed in the town for long.

### B1
Once upon a time, an old man and an old woman lived in a small village. They had no children. This made them feel very down every single day. One morning, snow fell on the ground. The couple looked outside from the house. The man suggested that they build a lovely snow child to bring a happy feeling. They went into the yard. They shaped the ice and snow with their hands. They gave her blue eyes, a little mouth, and hair made of gold. Suddenly, the snow girl came alive with a warm breath.

The happy parents named her Snegurochka. They loved her very much. She grew up quickly. She became more beautiful day by day. When spring arrived and the sun warmed the air, Snegurochka looked down. Summer came soon. It brought hot weather and flowers across the green fields. Other young people invited Snegurochka to join them for fun in the forest. Although she felt afraid, her mother told her that going outside was good. Her mother has always supported her in every way.

If she goes to the forest, she will have fun with the group. The group walked through the trees. They sang songs and played games. The children built a small fire. They started to jump over the warm light. Snegurochka stepped near the fire. She turned into water. She has left her parents with a deep feeling of sadness. They will always remember her sweet face. Life in the village went on quietly after that day.

The old man and woman had felt by themselves before they built the snow child. They wanted a child to share their home. The little girl brought light into their lives. Every single moment with her was special. She learned how to play games with the other kids in the town. Her mother always watched her with a careful eye. The sun became very hot as the weeks passed. The warm weather changed everything around them. If the sun gives a bright light, ice will always turn into water. That scientific fact became clear when Snegurochka got close to the fire.

The group of children cried when she turned into water. They ran back to the house to tell the parents. The mother and father came out quickly. They found only a small amount of water on the ground. Their hearts were full of heavy pain. They have kept her memory alive in their minds. The village remained quiet as time went on. People still talk about the wonderful snow girl. If anyone visits the village, they can hear the story. The old couple never forgot the sweet child they had loved so much.

### B2
Once upon a time, during a cold winter in a small village, an old man and an old woman lived together. They had no children, which made them feel very sad every single day. One morning, when the snow fell across the ground, the couple looked outside through the window. The man suggested that they should build a lovely snow child to bring some joy into their home. They went outside into the yard and began to shape the cold ice and snow with their hands. After they finished, they gave her two blue eyes, a little mouth, and hair made of gold. Suddenly, a special thing happened, and the snow girl came alive with a warm breath. The happy parents named her Snegurochka, and they loved her very much from that moment on. She grew up quickly, becoming more beautiful day by day, though she remained very quiet and pale. When spring arrived and the sun began to shine warmly, Snegurochka started to look sad and tired.

Summer came soon after, bringing hot weather that made the flowers grow across the green fields. Other young people in the village invited Snegurochka to join them for a fun day in the forest. Although she felt afraid, her mother told her that going outside with friends would be good for her. The group walked through the trees, singing songs and playing games as they enjoyed the nice afternoon. Later, the children built a small fire and started to jump over the warm fire for fun. Snegurochka tried to follow them, but as she stepped near the fire, she suddenly changed into a little cloud of water. Nothing was left behind except a light cloud that rose up into the sky. The old man and the old woman watched from their house with heavy hearts. They remembered the lovely snow child who had brought so much happiness into their lives. Though she was gone, her memory stayed with them through all the following seasons of the year.

Many months passed by after that strange summer day in the forest. The village experienced another cold winter with deep snow covering every street and roof. The old couple walked together in their yard where they had built the snow child before. They talked about the past and how much they missed their special daughter. Life in the small village continued as it always had done for many generations. People went about their daily business, taking care of their homes and families. Yet, the old man and the old woman never forgot the special moment when Snegurochka came alive. They knew that some experiences in life are too wonderful to ever fade away completely. Every time the snow fell during the cold months, they felt her presence close to their hearts. And so, the story of Snegurochka lived on in the quiet village through every generation.

Winter nights were long and dark, but the big room kept the small house warm. The couple often sat by the fire, speaking softly about their wonderful daughter. They imagined what she might be doing if she were still with them today. Sometimes the old man read a book while the old woman worked on her clothes. They tried to stay positive even though their home felt so quiet now. Friends from the village sometimes visited them to share food and talk about local news. These visits helped the couple feel less sad during the long winter evenings. They always thanked their good friends for bringing such support. Community life was important in the village, especially when the weather turned very cold. Everyone knew about the old couple and the special snow child they had loved.

As years passed, the old man and the old woman grew older and wiser. They accepted the natural order of the world with peace in their minds. The memory of Snegurochka remained a bright light in their shared history. Whenever children played outside in the snow, the couple watched them from the window. They could still see the image of their little snow girl in every white yard. No other child could ever take the place of Snegurochka in their hearts. The love they gave her during that single winter lasted for the rest of their lives. And when people in the village told the story to their own children, they spoke of love and wonder. The story of the snow girl became an important part of village history. Thus, the old man and the old woman found peace in knowing their story would live forever.

### C1
Once upon a time, during the dead of a merciless winter, an old man and an old woman lived together in a small, isolated village. They had no children, a silent sorrow that shadowed them through every waking hour and made the long evenings feel heavy. One bright morning, when the fresh snow had covered the ground in a smooth white blanket, the couple looked outside through their frosted window. The man suggested that they should build a lovely snow child to bring some warmth and joy into their quiet home. Without wasting a moment, they wrapped themselves in heavy coats and went outside into the frozen yard.

They began to shape the cold ice and packed snow with their gloved hands, working with a shared sense of hopeful purpose. After they finished forming the delicate shape, they gave her two pieces of blue glass for eyes, a little mouth, and hair made of golden straw. Suddenly, an impossible thing happened, and the cold snow girl came alive with a warm, gentle breath. The astonished parents named her Snegurochka, and they loved her very much from that very first magical moment. She grew up quickly under their care, becoming more graceful day by day, though she remained remarkably quiet and pale.

When spring finally arrived and the sun began to shine warmly upon the thawing earth, Snegurochka started to look sad and tired. She avoided the sunny spots in the room and preferred to sit near the dark, cool corners of the house. Summer came soon after, bringing hot weather that made the wild flowers grow across the green fields and forest edges. Other young people in the village invited Snegurochka to join them for a fun day of walking and exploring in the deep forest. Although she felt afraid of the bright light and the heat, her mother told her that going outside with friends would be good for her spirit.

The lively group walked beneath the tall trees, singing cheerful songs and playing games as they enjoyed the pleasant afternoon. Snegurochka tried to match their joyful steps, feeling for a brief moment like any other carefree youth in the village. Later in the day, the children built a small campfire in a clearing and started to jump over the warm flames for fun. Snegurochka tried to follow them, drawn by the laughter and the desire to belong completely to the group. As she stepped near the roaring fire, she suddenly changed into a little cloud of mist and sparkling water.

Nothing was left behind on the forest floor except a light, shining cloud that rose slowly up into the blue sky. The old man and the old woman watched this heartbreaking scene from their house with heavy, aching hearts. They remembered the lovely snow child who had brought so much unexpected happiness into their lonely lives. Though she was gone forever, her memory stayed with them through all the following seasons of the changing year. Their tears fell silently as they realized that their magical daughter belonged truly to the winter winds.

Many months passed by after that strange and sorrowful summer day in the quiet forest. The village experienced another severe winter with deep snow covering every street and wooden roof. The old couple walked slowly together in their empty yard where they had built the snow child so long ago. They talked about the past and how much they missed their special daughter during the long, freezing nights. Life in the small village continued as it always had done for many generations of hardworking people.

Villagers went about their daily business, taking care of their homes, feeding their animals, and supporting their families. Yet, the old man and the old woman never forgot the special moment when Snegurochka came alive before their eyes. They knew that some experiences in life are far too wonderful to ever fade away completely from human memory. Every time the snow fell heavily during the dark months, they felt her gentle presence close to their sorrowful hearts. And so, the story of Snegurochka lived on securely in the quiet village through every passing generation.

Winter nights were long and dark, but the big stone stove kept the small wooden house comfortably warm. The couple often sat by the crackling fire, speaking softly about their wonderful daughter and the joy she brought. They imagined what she might be doing or how she might look if she were still sitting with them today. Sometimes the old man read a book aloud while the old woman worked carefully on her sewing and winter clothes. They tried to stay positive and grateful even though their home felt so quiet and empty now.

Friends from the village sometimes visited them to share hot food and talk about local news and winter preparations. These warm visits helped the couple feel much less sad during the long and difficult winter evenings. They always thanked their good neighbors for bringing such steady support and kindness to their door. Community life was very important in the village, especially when the weather turned dangerously cold and stormy. Everyone in the settlement knew about the old couple and the special snow child they had loved so dearly.

As years passed steadily by, the old man and the old woman grew older and wiser with time. They accepted the natural order of the world with peace and quiet acceptance in their minds. The memory of Snegurochka remained a bright and guiding light in their shared history and memories. Whenever local children played outside in the fresh snow, the couple watched them tenderly from their frosted window. They could still see the clear image of their little snow girl in every white yard across the lane.

No other child could ever take the place of Snegurochka in the deep corners of their faithful hearts. The love they gave her during that single magical winter lasted for the rest of their long lives. And when people in the village told the story to their own children, they spoke of love, wonder, and loss. The story of the snow girl became an important and cherished part of local history and folklore. Thus, the old man and the old woman found lasting peace in knowing their story would live forever.

### B1
Once upon a time, an old man and an old woman lived together. They lived in a small house near a deep forest. The man built a wonderful thing for the woman because she wanted something special. They put liquid and land material over the structure so that it would be strong. They made it look like a farm animal to please her every single day. The man worked hard on the project for a long time. He used wood and other materials to make the body look right. The woman felt very happy when she saw the finished work. She thanked the man and gave him a warm hug. They kept the structure inside their home during the cold winter nights. They talked about how much they loved each other while sitting by the fire. Life was quiet and good for the old couple in their small home.

One fine morning, the woman took the structure out to the green field. She put it near the tall trees where the grass grew tall and green. She left it there while she worked on other things around the farm. She went back to the house to clean the rooms and prepare food. Forest animals came out from the dark woods because they felt interested in the new object. A big bear walked toward the structure and asked who it was. When the structure did not answer, the bear became angry. The bear tried to hit the structure with his hand. However, the hand of the bear stuck fast to the surface on the structure. The bear pulled and pulled, but he could not move away at all. He stayed right there because he was completely stuck to the side.

Soon after that, a gray animal came along and saw the bear stuck to the structure. The animal asked what was happening and tried to help his friend. He tried everything he knew, but his hands stayed completely fixed in place. Both creatures stood together and looked at each other with fear in their eyes. The old man and the old woman came outside to check on the field. They were very happy to find those animals there. They smiled when they saw the bear, the gray animal, and the smart visitor. They took the animals home and kept them safe inside a nice building. They gave the animals food and water to help them feel better. Every forest animal learned a good lesson about staying away from strange things in the field. From that day on, the old couple lived a good life with their new friends.

### B2

### C1
Once upon a time, nestled against the brooding perimeter of a deep forest, an old man and an old woman shared a modest dwelling. Despite the quiet rhythm of their days, the woman harbored a quiet yearning for something whimsical to break the monotony of rural life. To satisfy this wish, the man set to work with bundles of dry grass, carefully fashioning a remarkably convincing cow. He constructed the frame with great skill, reinforcing the delicate straw with a thick, sticky layer of pitch to ensure it possessed both strength and resilience. When the creation was finally complete, it stood surprisingly sturdy, ready to withstand whatever weather or whim might test it. The following morning, bathed in the soft amber light of dawn, the woman led the peculiar animal out to the verdant pasture bordering the trees. She positioned the grass beast where the rich meadow met the wild underbrush before returning to her domestic chores. Scarcely had she departed when the shadows of the forest stirred, yielding curious inhabitants drawn by the unfamiliar silhouette standing in the clearing. A formidable bear, lumbering out from the gloom of the ancient woods, approached the silent figure with guarded steps. Believing it to be a living rival, the massive beast demanded to know its identity and business in the territory. Receiving no reply save for the rustle of dry stems, the angry creature lashed out with a heavy paw to strike the impudent intruder. Instantly, his thick paw sank into the adhesive layer of pitch, sticking fast and robbing him of all mobility in a single careless movement.

Shortly thereafter, a sleek gray wolf emerged from the treeline, trotting briskly into the sunlit meadow. Catching sight of the mighty bear seemingly tethered to the strange golden creature, the wolf paused in sheer astonishment. Demanding an explanation for this undignified posture, the swift animal attempted to wrench his companion free with a sharp tug. Instead of liberating the bear, the wolf found his own paw hopelessly glued to the resinous surface, rendering him equally helpless. Before either captive could ponder their predicament, a brilliant red fox darted from the foliage with characteristic curiosity. Eager to investigate the bizarre tableau, the clever fox trotted up and addressed the two struggling prisoners with mocking amusement. Without pausing to test the surface himself, he brushed carelessly against the flank of the grass cow to offer a sarcastic push. In an instant, the fox joined his peers, completely immobilized by the tenacious compound. Thus, through no great exertion of their own, the clever old couple had ensnared several formidable denizens of the deep forest without firing a single shot. The old man and woman stepped out from the farmhouse later that afternoon, their faces lighting up with utter disbelief and joy. Recognizing their unexpected fortune, they approached the trapped trio with gentle determination rather than fear. They guided their captives away from the meadow, housing them securely within a sturdy wooden barn where escape was impossible.

Confined within the safety of the rustic enclosure, the forest creatures gradually abandoned their initial panic and hostility. They learned a profound, if humbling, lesson regarding the dangers of meddling blindly with unfamiliar objects in open fields. From that pivotal afternoon onward, the elderly couple settled into an existence characterized by a profound, unshakeable peace. Every sunrise offered a fresh panorama of emerald foliage and cerulean sky, promising yet another day of quiet harmony. While the woman tended the hearth and prepared nourishing meals, the man managed the daily labor of their modest farm. Often, as they worked side by side, they would converse in hushed, amused tones about the grand deception in the pasture. The captive bear and the gray wolf grew thoroughly accustomed to their new surroundings, shedding their wild anxieties entirely. Even the brilliant fox adapted with remarkable grace, finding unexpected contentment within the secure walls of the barn. No soul on the farm ever contemplated harming another creature, establishing a rare sanctuary of mutual respect and survival. Days dissolved into weeks, and the vibrant greens of summer slowly surrendered to the pristine white blankets of winter. Safe within their snug cottage, the small community listened to the howling gales without a shred of dread. The animals discovered new pastimes, playing simple games together during the long, frostbitten afternoons and finding solace in shared warmth.

During those extended winter hours, the clever fox entertained the group with countless agile tricks and clever antics. The bear and the wolf watched with genuine fascination, realizing that companionship far outweighed the bitter pride of the wild. Whenever the old man picked up his simple wooden flute, soft melodies filled the cozy interior of the farmhouse. The woman would hum along gently, her voice blending harmoniously with the notes as the animals drifted toward sleep. Nothing could fracture the resilient bond forged between the human inhabitants and their unconventional forest guests. As the seasons cycled onward, word of the remarkable straw ox spread far beyond the borders of the region. Curious travelers arrived from distant towns, eager to gaze upon the legendary creation that had outwitted the forest. The old man unfailingly recounted the tale to every arriving guest, gesturing toward the peaceful barn with quiet pride. Children from nearby hamlets played gleefully in the very pasture where the strange event had transpired years prior. They listened with wide eyes as the old man explained the mechanics of the sticky pitch that had secured such a famous prize. No one who heard the story ever forgot the wisdom of exercising caution and mindfulness before leaping into the unknown. The deep forest remained as ancient and quiet as ever, yet it now cradled a story of unexpected unity.

Nature continued to provide generously for the inhabitants of the farm, sustaining them through every shift of the calendar. The old couple strolled leisurely through the sunlit fields each afternoon, watching their former adversaries leap and chase one another. Grateful visitors frequently left tokens of appreciation, ranging from woven blankets to jars of sweet mountain honey. Laughter echoed effortlessly through the rooms, dispelling any lingering shadows from the past. The bright fox composed cheerful little tunes that resonated through the timber beams, amplifying the warmth of the hearth. Even the mighty bear learned the art of patience, sharing his portions willingly with the smaller animals in the barn. The gray wolf proved remarkably helpful, assisting the old man with hauling heavy logs for the winter hearth. Such devotion turned the modest homestead into a legendary haven of tranquility. As dusk descended upon the valley, the glowing windows of the cottage signaled safety to any weary traveler passing by. The old man and woman climbed the wooden stairs each night with untroubled minds, profoundly grateful for their peculiar adventure. They knew without a single doubt that their secluded refuge was the finest home in the entire world. Peace watched over them like a silent guardian, ensuring that their remaining years would be nothing short of extraordinary.
